Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in question answering, yet they still frequently suffer from hallucinations on knowledge-intensive tasks. Knowledge graphs (KGs) provide LLMs with structured, interpretable, and updatable factual grounding, making them a promising external knowledge source for reliable reasoning. However, existing LLM-guided graph reasoning methods typically rely on hop-wise greedy or beam-style pruning during evidence retrieval. Such local decision processes are inherently myopic: evidence that appears weak near the source may become crucial only after deeper graph context is explored, causing answer-critical branches to be discarded prematurely and making the reasoning chain difficult to recover. To address this limitation, we propose Foresight-over-Graph (FoG), a foresight-aware evidence retrieval framework for knowledge base question answering (KBQA). FoG iteratively constructs a question-relevant evidence subgraph and uses far-to-near feedback to guide path exploration, and maintains a compact memory subgraph to support continued exploration. Extensive experiments on widely used KBQA benchmarks demonstrate that FoG achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a particularly large improvement of 16.58% in Hit on CWQ, while also reducing LLM calls and token usage. Our code is available at https://github.com/yhong7/FoG .

</details>


### [35] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Cross-Domain and Multi-Turn Generalization of Latent Underspecification Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08413)
*Jerzy Kamiński,Ilya Galyukshev,Artem Kuznetsov,Danil Fedorov,Kirill Redko,Sergey Chuprin,Aidar Shumbalov,Stanislav Chumakov,Anna Kalyuzhnaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究大语言模型在对话中不可回答性检测的局限性：探针在跨数据集间具有较强迁移能力，但无法可靠提升实际任务性能，主要瓶颈在于模型对澄清问题的利用方式而非检测本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常在对话中过早回答或回答无法从给定信息中得出的内容，需要研究不可回答性在不同形式间是否共享表示，以及该信号在对话中的有用性。

Method: 构建了包含423个对话、1661个标注回合状态的基准测试，设计了模拟用户的评估框架，使用六个数据集和六个开放权重LLM，测试探针在不可回答性检测中的表现及迁移能力。

Result: 探针在共享不可回答性基础的数据集间迁移稳健（数学和文本缺失信息AUROC 0.77-0.97，SQuAD 2.0与MuSiQue 0.77-0.90）；"已知未知"探针迁移较差但受词汇控制影响；单轮探针无法零样本检测对话何时变得可回答；校准探针门控在欠具体回合上触发精度高于随机，但无法可靠超越基线生成或提示式整合；任务成功率仅比真实标签门控低0.08。

Conclusion: 检测能力无法可靠提升实际任务性能，主要差距在于模型如何利用澄清信息而非检测本身。

Abstract: Large language models routinely answer questions that cannot be answered from the information given, and in dialogue they answer before enough has been said. Unanswerability is linearly decodable from hidden states, but it is unclear which of its forms share a representation and whether the signal is useful in dialogue. We contribute a turn-labeled multi-turn benchmark (423 conversations, 1,661 labeled turn-states) and an evaluation harness with a simulated user who answers clarifying questions, and use them with six datasets and six open-weight LLMs to test how far probes for unanswerability carry. Probes transfer robustly between datasets that share a ground of unanswerability: missing information in math (AUROC 0.77-0.97) and in a passage (SQuAD 2.0<->MuSiQue, 0.77-0.90). Probes for epistemic "known-unknowns" transfer poorly to math, but this separation weakens under lexical controls and changes with layer and coordinate system, so it remains unresolved. Single-turn probes fail zero-shot to detect when a conversation becomes answerable; in-structure probes recover it, but no better than a bag-of-words classifier. A gate on the calibrated probe, with no model fine-tuning, fires on underspecified turns far more precisely than chance, and its end-task success comes within 0.08 of a gate given the true labels. Yet across four models it does not reliably beat vanilla generation or prompted consolidation. The remaining gap lies mostly in how models use a clarification, not in detection.

</details>

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own generations using teacher feedback. With different tokenizers, comparing teacher and student predictions requires alignment at both sequence and vocabulary levels. In this paper, we examine whether expanding this alignment coverage improves learning. Across three heterogeneous teacher--student pairs on mathematical reasoning and code generation, strict 1:1 groups already cover most student-generated tokens despite substantial vocabulary mismatch. On responses sampled from the students before distillation, the shared vocabulary retains nearly all teacher and student probability mass at strictly aligned positions on average. Restricting reverse KL to a student-selected top-16 subset of the shared vocabulary at each strict position achieves accuracy comparable to full shared-vocabulary OPD, outperforming the evaluated cross-tokenizer baselines. Adding mean squared error supervision on span log-probabilities in mismatch groups gives complete supervision coverage, yet reduces accuracy. At checkpoints from training with only the strict loss, the span gradients show weak or negative directional agreement with the strict gradients and grow in magnitude relative to them. These diagnostics may help explain the accuracy drop from adding span supervision. Our findings motivate a shift from maximizing alignment coverage to prioritizing supervision reliability: compact supervision at strict positions can be more effective than broader coverage that introduces weakly aligned or conflicting training signals.

</details>

*Lasse B. Strand,Robert Jakob,Kevin O'Sullivan,Markus Kreft*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Agentic AutoRAG，一种基于LLM代理的多目标RAG超参数优化框架，通过诊断器区分检索与生成失败原因，在保证准确率的同时显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG管道配置是昂贵的超参数优化问题，现有优化器无法建模配置失败原因，无法充分利用检索到的证据信息。

Method: Agentic AutoRAG包含两个核心组件：1)诊断器，根据检索到的块将失败问题归因于检索或生成阶段；2)提议者，基于模型排名和定价知识库，在准确率和成本间权衡以追踪帕累托前沿。采用冻结考试评估配置，多目标优化。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准测试中，LLM评判准确率超过所有对比基线；前10次试验达到或超过基线30次试验的准确率；在真实医疗语料库成本感知模式下，达到77%准确率（比最强基线71.5%更高），成本仅为基线的58%，或在22%成本下达到相同准确率。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了将失败归因机制融入RAG优化的重要性，实现了在准确率和成本之间的有效权衡，为实际应用提供了高性价比的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a widely used approach for grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge. However, configuring a pipeline is an expensive hyperparameter optimization problem over many interacting choices, from chunking and embedding model to reranking and generation. Existing optimizers, from greedy search to Bayesian optimization, reduce each trial to an aggregate score and search without modeling why a configuration performed as it did, even though the retrieved chunks already provide evidence about whether each failure occurred during retrieval or after it. We introduce Agentic AutoRAG, an LLM-agent optimizer for multi-objective RAG hyperparameter optimization with retrieval-versus-generation failure attribution. It proposes configurations scored on a frozen exam from the corpus: after each trial a Diagnoser attributes each failed question to retrieval or generation, and a Proposer, grounded in a knowledge base of model rankings and pricing, selects the next configuration, weighing accuracy against cost to trace a Pareto frontier. On three multi-hop QA benchmarks it reaches higher LLM-judge accuracy than every baseline we compare, and within its first 10 trials it matches or beats the statistical baselines' full 30-trial judge accuracy. In its cost-aware mode on a real-world healthcare corpus it reaches a median exam accuracy of 77%, above the strongest baseline's 71.5%, at about 58% of that baseline's cost per query, and it matches that 71.5% at about 22% of the cost.

</details>

Abstract: Long-context inference and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) handle evidence selection at vastly different scales, from a single long prompt to an entire corpus. We ask whether a single model-internal mechanism can select evidence across this range. We introduce UNifying REtrieval And Long-Context with a Single Model (UNREAL), a model-native evidence selection framework to span corpus retrieval and long-context inference. UNREAL encodes chunks and derives retrieval queries directly from the frozen LLM's internal representations. It adds fewer than 500K trainable parameters and leaves the backbone unchanged. On a 3B-token, 21M-chunk Wikipedia index, all four dense and hybrid UNREAL backbones outperform state-of-the-art retriever-reranker systems. The best model raises recall from 49.1% to 73.2% on HotpotQA, from 31.7% to 60.1% on 2WikiMultiHopQA, and from 8.8% to 14.4% on MuSiQue. Applied to long-context tasks, the same selection mechanism removes distractors before generation, raising NoLiMa accuracy from 1.0% to 24.83% at its maximum context length of 128K tokens, and LV-Eval's F1 score from 49.97% to 54.66% at 256K. UNREAL also reduces FLOPs and time-to-first-token relative to full-context inference from roughly 32K tokens onward, with larger gains as context grows. Together, these results establish model-internal evidence selection as a common foundation for corpus retrieval and evidence-sparse long-context inference.

</details>


### [39] [Wiki-Talkie: Multilingual Benchmarking of Persona-Based Agents on Real-World Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08513)
*Dennis Fucci,Andrea Bacciu,Dong Liu,Weronika Łajewska,Saab Mansour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Wiki-Talkie多语言真实对话数据集，评估LLM代理模拟人类交互的行为保真度，发现用户评论历史优于显式人物信息，但模型存在系统性偏见


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自主代理在社会环境中的应用日益增多，需要研究其模拟人类交互的忠实度。现有数据集依赖虚构人物且语言有限，缺乏跨不同人群的行为保真度评估所需的经验基础

Method: 构建Wiki-Talkie数据集：包含德语、英语、西班牙语、法语、意大利语五个语言的真实维基百科讨论页面对话，配以从真实用户社区派生的人物画像（社会人口属性、自我描述、行为导向的交互特征）。在下一步生成任务上评估代理交互行为，比较不同人物条件化策略

Result: 用户评论历史（展示交互行为）优于显式人物信息。模型系统性低估负面或极端情感，过度产生引用和建议，表现出对宜人性和积极性的偏见。这些模式在语言间保持稳健，仅有小的跨语言差异

Conclusion: LLM代理在模拟真实人类交互时存在系统性偏见，用户行为历史比显式人物信息更能指导代理生成。需要改进人物 grounding 策略以提升跨语言、跨人群的行为保真度

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in social environments, making it critical to study their ability to faithfully simulate human interactions. Central to this is grounding agents in realistic user personas, yet existing datasets rely on fictional personas and are limited to a handful of languages, lacking the empirical grounding necessary to evaluate behavioral fidelity across diverse populations. We introduce Wiki-Talkie, a multilingual dataset of real-world conversations from Wikipedia Talk pages across five languages spanning two language families: Germanic (German, English) and Romance (Spanish, French, Italian), paired with personas derived from real user communities and encompassing sociodemographic attributes, self-descriptions, and behaviorally grounded interaction traits. Using Wiki-Talkie, we evaluate agent interactional behavior on a next-turn generation task across various persona conditioning strategies. Our evaluation assesses whether agents collectively reproduce the distributional behavioral patterns observed in human discussions. Results show that user's comment history exemplifying interaction behavior consistently outperforms explicit persona information. In addition, models systematically underproduce negative or extreme sentiments, while over producing references and suggestions, revealing biases toward agreeableness and positivity. Crucially, these patterns hold robustly across languages, with small cross-lingual differences.

</details>


### [40] [Latent space bias directions in LLMs capture confidence, not fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08559)
*Stephanie Buttigieg,Maeve Madigan,Parameswaran Kamalaruban,Stuart Burrell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析激活引导（activation steering）去除大模型偏见的原理，发现引导向量主要编码的是模型置信度而非偏见本身。通过对比有偏见和无偏见提示词的激活值，发现引导方向指向低概率token而非偏见表示。沿该方向引导确实降低了测量到的偏见，但这源于降低模型置信度而非纠正底层偏好，导致模型在QA任务中倾向于放弃回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明激活引导泛化性差且存在意外效果，作者旨在分析引导方向实际编码了什么，以揭示其不一致的性能表现。

Method: 通过对比有偏见和无偏见提示词的激活值提取线性引导方向，并在偏见和通用知识基准上评估该引导干预的效果。

Result: 引导方向主要由模型置信度主导，指向激活空间中从高概率到低概率token的区域，而非编码有意义的偏见表示。沿该方向引导确实降低偏见测量值，但这是因为降低了模型置信度，导致模型在QA基准上倾向于放弃回答。

Conclusion: 模型置信度是隐藏空间中有偏见和无偏见提示词的主要区分因素，分离出与置信度无关的偏见表示很难。激活引导去偏见结果应谨慎解读，其减少偏见并非通过纠正底层偏好，而是通过降低置信度，甚至对与偏见无关的任务也有影响。

Abstract: Activation steering has gained popularity as a lightweight inference-time debiasing technique for large language models. However, prior work reports that steering vectors generalise poorly, with unintended effects on model performance and limited transfer to new datasets. Our work analyses what the debiasing direction used for activation steering actually encodes, in order to shed light on its inconsistent performance. We study the linear debiasing direction obtained by contrasting the activations of anti-biased and biased prompts, and evaluate it as a steering intervention across bias and general knowledge benchmarks. We find that this direction is dominated by model confidence, pointing from regions of high to low-probability tokens in activation space rather than encoding a meaningful representation of model bias. Steering along it does reduce measured bias, but this is a consequence of reducing model confidence: on QA benchmarks we find that this steering drives the model to abstain from answering, with a side effect of improving fairness metrics. Our experiments show that model confidence is the dominant separating factor between biased and anti-biased prompts in hidden space, indicating that isolating a linear representation of bias which is disentangled from model confidence is difficult and steering-based debiasing results should be interpreted with care. In short, steering appears to reduce bias, not by correcting the model's underlying preferences, but by making it less confident, even on tasks unrelated to bias.

</details>


### [41] [Incidental information contaminates patient notes and disrupts clinical reasoning in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08585)
*Krithik Vishwanath,Brandon Ye,Anton Alyakin,John E. Markert,Aaron Hsieh,Michał Mańkowski,Eric K. Oermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究调查了大型语言模型在临床环境中的意外信息敏感性。研究发现，前沿模型在35%的病历中插入了闲聊内容，在3.7%的病历中错误归属或临床化使用了无关信息。背景噪音导致48.2%的转录包含污染，4个开放权重模型中有5.3%的生成笔记被污染。研究提出双编码假设，即支持临床推理的组件也容易受意外信息干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于支持临床文档记录和临床推理，但存在一个共同的失败模式：对与患者就诊无关的意外信息的敏感性。需要评估这一风险以确定LLM在临床应用中的安全性。

Method: 进行了两项主要研究：1) 分析576次患者-临床医生对话，评估前沿模型插入闲聊和错误归属信息的频率；2) 在57次模拟记录咨询中测试背景语音污染。使用4个开放权重模型进行下游笔记生成测试。

Result: 1) 前沿模型在35%的病历中插入闲聊，质量评分变化不超过0.20分；2) 在3.7%的病历中错误归属或临床化使用无关信息；3) 48.2%的转录包含背景噪音污染；4) 5.3%的下游笔记被污染。初步证据表明支持临床推理的组件也容易受意外信息干扰。

Conclusion: 应在使用前评估LLM对意外信息的抵抗力，实施防止污染同时保留临床推理能力的防护措施。双编码假设表明临床推理和干扰机制在LLM中可能共享组件，这为改进LLM临床应用的安全性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly relied upon to support ambient documentation and clinical reasoning. Here we examine the impact of a failure mode shared between these two applications by assessing their sensitivity to information incidental to the patient encounter. In 576 patient-clinician dialogues, we found that frontier models inserted small-talk exchanges into 35% of notes, while mean quality scores changed by at most 0.20 points on five-point scales. In 3.7% of frontier notes, models misattributed the asides or used them clinically. In 57 mock recorded consultations, background speech from a separate patient encounter at -10 dB leaked into 48.2% of transcripts, with contamination detected in 5.3% of downstream notes generated by four open-weight models. We propose a dual encoding hypothesis of clinical reasoning and distraction in LLMs, with preliminary evidence that LLM components associated with disruption by incidental information also support clinical reasoning. These findings support evaluating resistance to incidental information before clinical use, with safeguards that prevent contamination while preserving clinical reasoning.

</details>


### [42] [Towards In-Parameter Memory Augmentation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08630)
*Haoyu Huang,Zhongwei Xie,Jiaxin Bai,Yisen Gao,Hong Ting Tsang,Wuganjing Song,Huihao Jing,Yufei Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了在LLM部署阶段引入参数化记忆的方法，通过将记忆信息编码到模型参数或适配器中，以补充上下文学习并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练后需要融入新知识（如领域事实、用户偏好、文档等），但传统的上下文学习（ICL）消耗上下文容量且存在重复离散编码成本。参数化记忆提供了一种互补方案，将可复用记忆信息嵌入模型参数，可在推理时动态组合。

Method: 从两个正交维度组织研究格局：1）参数放置位置（Embedding、Attention、FFN层或混合层）；2）参数获取时机（在线部署时获取 vs 预部署时获取）。对方法边界进行澄清、比较，并讨论干扰、安全性、与ICL协同设计及递归自我改进等方向。

Result: 系统梳理了在LLM部署阶段引入参数化记忆的方法分类与边界，明确了不同参数放置位置与获取时机之间的关系，并对现有方法的局限性和潜在研究方向进行了分析。

Conclusion: 参数化记忆为LLM提供了灵活且高效的动态知识注入机制，未来可在干扰控制、安全性、与ICL协同设计及递归自我改进等方面进一步探索。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents increasingly need to incorporate knowledge acquired after pretraining, e.g., domain facts, user preferences, documents, and interaction experience. In-context learning (ICL) and ICL-based agent harness remain flexible, but they consume context capacity and incur repeated discretized encoding cost that grows with context length. \textbf{In-parameter memory} offers a complementary substrate: reusable memory information is represented in model parameters, adapters, or other parameter-like objects that are composed into the forward pass at inference time. This survey focuses on methods that augment LLMs with such parametric memory at deployment: a memory-bearing parameter object is plugged into the forward pass during inference, whether it is acquired before or during deployment. We organize the landscape with two orthogonal axes: \textbf{Parameter Placement}, which includes Embedding, Attention, FFN layers, or Hybrid when two or more layers are used; and \textbf{Parameter Acquisition Time}, which distinguishes methods whose memory object is acquired during deployment (online) from those acquired before it (offline). We clarify boundaries, conduct comparisons, and discuss open directions in interference, safety, co-design with ICL, and recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [43] [Evidence-Bound Reasoning: Neuro-Semantic Verification of Biomedical AI in Glioblastoma Radiogenomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08660)
*Mariya Miteva,Maria Nisheva-Pavlova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经语义验证框架，将放射组学测量转换为可验证的证据记录和可机器检查的声明，实现了预测性能与可验证性的解耦。该框架在跨队列移植、LLM声明提取和受控预测退化测试中均表现出色，验证准确率始终保持100%，而预测性能可独立调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学AI在生成解释时往往缺乏对患者特定证据的可靠验证。研究者开发了一种神经语义验证框架，旨在将放射组学测量转换为可验证的证据记录和可机器检查的声明，解决AI解释缺乏可靠性的问题。

Method: 研究将UPenn-GBM放射组学数据与de novo CaPTk从标准化MRI提取的数据以及独立多中心队列中专家验证的分割结果对齐。共享空间包含来自T1、T1GD、T2和FLAIR MRI在三个肿瘤区域的1,728个特征。通过611例UPenn病例定义参考语义状态，评估了跨队列移植性、模型关联溯源、确定性验证、受控预测退化和LLM声明提取试点。MGMT预测仅作为移植压力测试。

Result: 语义状态平均一致性为0.786（加权kappa 0.709），形态学特征一致性最高(0.918)，强度特征最低(0.252)。外部证据账本包含331名患者的1,655条模型关联记录。验证器在6,620条声明污染基准测试中达到100%精确集合准确率。在24例试点中，GPT-5.6 Sol重现了72/72条预定义原子声明，冻结验证器恢复了24/24条预期条件。受控退化期间，ROC AUC从0.899降至0.500，但验证准确率保持1.000。外部MGMT鉴别能力较弱(ROC AUC 0.543)。

Conclusion: 可验证性可以独立于预测性能进行工程化和评估。LLM可以构建解释，但最终证据一致性检查仍需确定性验证。验证框架表明，即使预测性能下降，验证准确率仍可保持100%，证明了可验证性与预测性能可以解耦。

Abstract: Background: Biomedical AI can generate plausible explanations without reliably verifying whether each statement is supported by patient-specific evidence. We developed a neuro-semantic verification framework that converts radiomic measurements into addressable evidence records and machine-checkable claims. Methods: UPenn-GBM radiomics were aligned with de novo CaPTk extraction from standardized MRI and expert-validated segmentations in an independent multicenter cohort. The shared space comprised 1,728 features from T1, T1GD, T2, and FLAIR MRI across three tumor regions. Reference-defined semantic states were derived from 611 UPenn cases. We evaluated cross-cohort transportability, model-linked provenance, deterministic verification, controlled predictive degradation, and an LLM claim-extraction pilot; MGMT prediction served only as a transport stress test. Results: Median semantic-state agreement was 0.786 (weighted kappa 0.709), ranging from 0.918 for morphologic to 0.252 for intensity features. The external evidence ledger contained 1,655 model-linked records for 331 patients. The verifier achieved 100% exact-set accuracy in a 6,620-claim corruption benchmark. In a 24-case pilot, GPT-5.6 Sol reproduced 72/72 prespecified atomic claims, and the frozen verifier recovered 24/24 expected conditions. During controlled degradation, ROC AUC declined from 0.899 to 0.500 while verification accuracy remained 1.000. External MGMT discrimination was weak (ROC AUC 0.543). Conclusions: Verifiability can be engineered and evaluated independently of predictive performance. LLMs may structure explanations, while final evidence-consistency checking remains deterministic.

</details>


### [44] [Agreement Is Not Validity: Cross-Model LLM Consensus in Diagnosing Student Failure Modes in K-12 Math Tutoring Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08703)
*Clayton Cohn,Joyce Fonteles,Kirk Vanacore,Gianni Mazza,Candida Crawford,Tom Hooper,Gautam Biswas,Rene Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索了大型语言模型（LLM）在K-12数学辅导对话中对五种学生失败模式进行分类的效度。研究发现，模型间一致性较高但人类-模型一致性中等，表明模型共识不能替代独立证据来证明学习分析的效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析研究日益依赖LLM从学生-辅导对话中提取信息以进行知识追踪、行为建模和错误诊断，但LLM生成解释的效度尚未得到充分理解。

Method: 采用操作性诊断分类法书，研究LLM对五种数学辅导对话中的学生失败模式（不确定性、错误归因、算子选择、概念差距、程序性失误）的分类。测量了人类-模型一致性和跨模型一致性，使用 kappa 系数和 alpha 系数进行分析。

Result: 跨模型一致性显著高于人类-模型一致性（kappa = .755-.781; alpha = .769 对比 .524-.597）。高模型共识可能产生虚假的正确性外观。

Conclusion: 模型共识不能替代效度的独立证据。对于学习分析而言，可扩展的标注只有在其推断的构念有效时才有用，模型共识不能证明其效度。

Abstract: In K-12 mathematics tutoring, student-tutor dialogue provides rich evidence of learners' problem-solving processes and sources of difficulty. Learning analytics research increasingly relies on large language models (LLMs) to extract such information from dialogue for a variety of downstream tasks, including knowledge tracing, behavioral modeling, and diagnosis of student reasoning errors. However, the validity of these model-generated interpretations remains insufficiently understood. In this exploratory study, we examine the validity of LLM classifications of five student failure modes in mathematics tutoring dialogue using an operational diagnostic codebook: uncertainty, misattribution, operator selection, conceptual gap, and procedural slip. Across models, human-LLM agreement was moderate (kappa = .524-.597), while cross-model agreement was substantially higher (kappa = .755-.781; alpha = .769). These findings show that cross-model agreement can create a misleading appearance of correctness, challenging the assumption that consensus among LLMs constitutes evidence of valid learner interpretation. For learning analytics, the implication is clear: scalable labeling is useful only if the inferred constructs are valid, and model consensus cannot substitute for independent evidence of that validity.

</details>


### [45] [IdeaAnchor: Teaching LLMs to Turn Literature into Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08781)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Jiashuo Sun,Yiling Ma,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IdeaAnchor是一种基于结构化规范（即“锚点”）来训练大语言模型进行文献驱动的科研创意生成的范式。通过从已发表论文中挖掘创意生成实例，利用演示、自蒸馏和强化学习等方法训练模型，使模型能够理解论文的功能角色、关系和目标合成标准。实验表明，结合锚点训练和检索增强的方案在创意生成质量上效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示或反馈的语言模型训练方法在文献驱动的科研创意生成方面存在不足，缺乏对论文如何进行结构化合成的监督信号，导致模型难以学习如何从多篇论文中有效整合信息并产生创新性想法。

Method: IdeaAnchor范式通过挖掘已发表论文中的创意生成实例，构建包含论文功能角色、关系及目标合成标准的结构化规范（“锚点”）。利用演示学习、自蒸馏和强化学习进行模型训练，并在推理时引入检索增强来提升生成质量。

Result: 实验结果显示该模型在科研创意生成任务上取得了持续的性能提升。消融分析表明：基于锚点的训练强化了创意的综合能力，检索增强提升了细节丰富度，二者结合能取得最佳效果。

Conclusion: 通过引入结构化的“锚点”作为特权信号，IdeaAnchor有效解决了大语言模型在文献驱动科研创意生成中的监督缺失问题，证明了结构化指导和检索增强相结合是提升模型创意生成质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Scientific research often begins by synthesizing ideas from a set of related papers to identify gaps and formulate new directions. However, training language models to perform this form of literature-grounded ideation remains challenging, as existing approaches based on prompting or feedback lack structured supervision for how papers should be synthesized. We introduce IdeaAnchor, a paradigm for training LLMs to perform research ideation using structured specifications as privileged signals. Each IdeaAnchor instance encodes how each input paper should be synthesized into a successful idea, including their functional roles, relationships, and target synthesis criteria. We build this paradigm by mining instances from published papers, capturing how real ideas emerge from prior literature. We then train models via demonstration, self-distillation, and reinforcement learning, and further enhance generation with retrieval at inference time. Experiments show consistent improvements in ideation quality. Our analysis reveals a functional decomposition: anchor-based training strengthens creative synthesis, retrieval enhances detail elaboration, and combining both yields the best performance.

</details>


### [46] [How Much Do LLM-as-a-Judge Design Choices Matter? A Systematic Comparison of Prompt Designs, Rating Scales, and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05094)
*Laurène Vaugrante,Thilo Hagendorff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究LLM作为评估器的设计选择如何影响评估结果，发现模型身份是主要方差来源，设计选择会产生显著影响，为LLM-as-a-judge方法学提供参考


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评估器缺乏设计标准，研究者随意选择提示词、评分尺度等，可能导致不同研究结论不一致，需要提供实证基础来指导法官设计

Method: 在两个任务上评估10个推理模型：句子情感和毒性评分（500+项）、问答对二分类准确率（600项），测试不同设计选择的影响

Result: 评分任务中LLM法官与人类基准有显著差异但绝对差异小（0.11分），毒性法官优于标准分类器，分类任务准确率96.5%；设计选择可转移偏差0.93分，影响法官宽容度（详细提示词下降28.9%，模型切换下降56.1%）；模型身份是主要方差来源

Conclusion: LLM法官总体值得信赖，但设计选择会产生显著方差，为LLM-as-a-judge方法学提供了重要参考，强调设计选择的重要性

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use Large Language Models as judges (LLM-as-a-judge) to evaluate model outputs. Yet there are no standards for how to design these judges. Typically, researchers choose the prompt, rating scale, and model intuitively. If these choices change the judge's verdicts, two studies can reach different conclusions about the same facts. To address this risk and to provide an empirical basis for judge designs, we evaluate 10 reasoning models across multiple designs on two tasks: a scalar rating of sentence sentiment and toxicity (over 500 items per category), as well as a binary accuracy classification of question-answer pairs (n=600). For the rating tasks, despite judges showing significant disagreements with the human ground truth, the practical size of differences is small enough to consider most judges reliable (mean absolute deviation of 0.11 points on a 1 - 7 scale); toxicity judges even outperform standard classifiers. Judges are also highly accurate on average (96.5%) for the accuracy classification task. However, design choices can produce shifts: changing the rating scale alone can shift measured bias by up to 0.93 points (rating task), and while accuracy levels are rarely impacted, design choices consistently impact judge leniency (classification task; leniency drop of 28.9 percentage points when using detailed prompts, and up to 56.1 percentage points when switching models). Counterintuitively, lower reasoning effort affects neither accuracy nor leniency. Across both tasks, model identity is the dominant source of variance. These findings suggest that while LLM judges are broadly trustworthy in aggregate, design choices can be meaningful sources of variance. Given the growing reliance on automated evaluation in LLM research, we intend this study as a methodological reference for designing more robust and replicable LLM-as-a-judge pipelines.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [47] [GAMEGO: Training Game-Dev Agents with Synthetic Trajectories Anchored in Real-World Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06910)
*Haoyue Yang,Jingyao Li,Zhengfan Wu,Jing Liu,Xuanle Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GameGo是一个可扩展框架，通过任务特定动态压缩将简短游戏种子转换为基于行业游戏开发实践的全面产品需求文档，并训练GameGoCoder模型进行游戏生成，在多个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型在网页前端执行方面展现出强大能力，但直接从稀疏用户查询生成复杂游戏往往迫使编码代理做出欠明确的假设，导致游戏机制不完整、游戏流程脱节以及视觉美学受限等问题。

Method: GameGo框架采用任务特定动态压缩技术来最大化信息密度同时保持指令遵循能力，将简短游戏种子转换为基于行业游戏开发实践的全面产品需求文档。同时构建了GameGoData（包含55,060个跨越2D、2.5D和3D游戏的发展轨迹）和GameGoBench（包含124个多样化游戏查询的基准测试）。

Result: 在GameGoData上训练的GameGoCoder模型在匹配基准测试中优于匹配的基线模型，在游戏开发基准测试中可与前沿模型相媲美。

Conclusion: 所有代码、数据集和模型都将公开提供。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in web front-end execution, with browser-based game generation emerging as a particularly prominent frontier. While previous efforts frequently rely on complex multi-turn workflows or focus on static game evaluation benchmarks, this work targets direct end-to-end real-world game synthesis driven by coding agents. However, generating complex games directly from sparse user queries often forces coding agents to make underspecified assumptions, yielding incomplete mechanics, disconnected gameplay flows, and limited visual aesthetics. To resolve this issue, this paper presents GameGo, a scalable framework that systematically transforms brief game seeds into comprehensive Product Requirements Documents grounded in industry game-development practices. To retain core gameplay constraints without restricting design exploration, GameGo uses task-specific dynamic compression to maximize information density while preserving instruction following. Based on this pipeline, GameGoData is constructed with 55,060 development trajectories across 2D, 2.5D, and 3D games, alongside GameGoBench, a benchmark comprising 124 diverse game queries. Training GameGoCoder on GameGoData yields a model that outperforms matched baselines and is comparable to frontier models across gamedev benchmarks. All code, datasets, and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [48] [FluidPD: In-Place Elasticity for SLO-Aware Prefill-Decode Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06917)
*Kartik Ramesh,Kaidi Fu,Zihan Zheng,Jiahuan Yu,Fabio Oliveira,Carlos Costa,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FluidPD是面向LLM服务中prefill-decode解耦架构的SLO感知就地弹性系统，通过FluidToken处理瞬时不平衡、FluidRole处理持续不平衡，实现无需额外worker即可大幅提升SLO达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有prefill-decode解耦LLM服务系统使用固定worker比例，而真实工作负载呈现prefill-to-decode需求比的短时突发和持续波动；固定配置会导致配置不匹配，即使存在空闲容量也会引发SLO违规；现有自动扩缩容机制反应慢、需要备用GPU且无法解决短时相位不平衡问题。

Method: FluidPD提出两个互补机制：1) FluidToken：当decode侧有冗余时将有界prefill计算卸载到decode worker，处理瞬时不平衡；2) FluidRole：原地重新分配running worker在prefill和decode角色间，避免模型重载和引擎重启，处理持续不平衡。两者均由轻量级压力指标引导，在SLO违规前暴露prefill和decode侧资源压力。

Result: 在生产Azure追踪工作负载上，FluidPD相比静态SGLang在整体SLO达成率上提升高达94.6个百分点，证明SLO感知就地P/D弹性能在无需额外worker的情况下改善服务质量。

Conclusion: SLO感知就地prefill-decode弹性机制可显著提升服务质量，且无需额外worker资源即可实现。

Abstract: Prefill-decode disaggregation is becoming a common architecture for LLM serving because it separates two phases with distinct execution patterns and SLO objectives. Existing systems typically combine a fixed prefill/decode worker ratio with request routing across workers. However, real-world workloads exhibit both short bursts and sustained shifts in the prefill-to-decode demand ratio. As a result, a configuration that is well provisioned at one time may quickly become mismatched, causing latency SLO violations even when idle capacity exists elsewhere. Existing autoscaling mechanisms can add capacity, but they react slowly, require spare GPUs, and do not directly address short-timescale phase imbalance.
  We present FluidPD, a P/D-disaggregated serving system that provides SLO-aware in-place elasticity. FluidPD introduces two complementary mechanisms. FluidToken handles transient imbalance by offloading a bounded portion of prefill computation to decode workers when decode-side slack is available. FluidRole handles sustained imbalance by reassigning running workers between prefill and decode roles in place, avoiding model reload and engine restart. Both mechanisms are guided by lightweight pressure indices that expose prefill and decode-side resource pressure before they appear as SLO violations. Across production Azure trace workloads, FluidPD improves overall SLO attainment over static SGLang by up to 94.6 percentage points, demonstrating that SLO-aware in-place P/D elasticity improves service quality without provisioning additional workers.

</details>


### [49] [RadOnc-Agent: An LLM-Orchestrated Framework for AI Workflows Across the Radiotherapy Care Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06923)
*Caiwen Jiang,Shuoyang Wei,Songlin Zhao,Junyu Li,Jingyuan Chen,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadOnc-Agent是一种基于大语言模型的代理式AI框架，通过对话接口提供26个可调用函数，将放射治疗划分为四个临床阶段，在长程治疗工作流中协调异构的放射治疗能力和信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射治疗中的AI能力目前分散在不同的临床阶段、软件环境和数据模态中，与从治疗决策到随访的长程放射治疗工作流不匹配。研究旨在构建一个统一的框架来协调异构的放射治疗能力。

Method: RadOnc-Agent框架包含：1）将放射治疗划分为四个临床阶段；2）通过对话接口提供26个可调用函数；3）使用大语言模型控制器将临床意图映射到模式约束的调用；4）保持患者和工作流上下文；5）将请求路由到专业服务。

Result: 在2,600次单函数执行（7,800次重复）、200个跨阶段场景（600次执行）和120个工作流实例（360次执行）的评估中，系统表现：单函数执行准确率98.79%，脚本化跨阶段工作流完成率96.50%，真实患者工作流完成率96.67%。消融研究显示：移除纵向状态会使跨阶段完成率降至84.00%；禁用模式和身份验证会使后端分发错误率从0%升至95.28%。

Conclusion: 研究证实了LLM编排架构在长程放射治疗工作流中协调异构能力的可行性，但未证明临床正确性、临床效用或前瞻性获益。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has advanced individual radiotherapy tasks, yet these capabilities remain separated across clinical stages, software environments and data modalities. This fragmentation contrasts with the longitudinal radiotherapy workflow from treatment decision-making through follow-up. Here we present RadOnc-Agent, an agentic artificial-intelligence framework that formalizes radiotherapy into four clinical phases and provides 26 callable functions through a conversational interface. A large-language-model controller maps clinical intent to schema-constrained calls, preserves patient and workflow context, and routes requests to specialist services. We evaluated system execution using 2,600 single-function requests (7,800 repeat executions), 200 prespecified synthetic cross-stage scenarios spanning four phases (600 executions), and 120 workflow instances from 60 de-identified patient records (360 clean executions) representing decision-to-planning and planning-to-adaptation. RadOnc-Agent selected the intended function in 98.79% of single-function executions, completed 96.50% of scripted cross-stage workflows, and completed 96.67% of real-patient workflow executions. In comparative ablations, removing longitudinal state reduced cross-stage completion from 96.50% to 84.00%, while disabling schema and identity validation increased mismatched backend dispatch from 0% to 95.28% in a replay/test evaluation. These findings establish the technical feasibility of an LLM-orchestrated architecture for coordinating heterogeneous radiotherapy capabilities and information across longitudinal workflows; they do not establish clinical correctness, clinical utility or prospective benefit.

</details>


### [50] [Principles that Guide, Actions that Inform: Agent Evolution via Knowledge Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06964)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型代理在持续进化能力方面的局限，提出SAGA框架，通过执行-抽象反馈循环将交互轨迹转化为可重用的抽象知识，从而提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖参数微调或仅关注经验表征，导致知识高度依赖特定任务，难以实现跨情境泛化；关键挑战在于如何将具体交互转化为可指导未来决策的抽象且可重用的知识。

Method: SAGA框架包含三个阶段：1）将交互轨迹渐进式转换为情境描述、可重用程序及带适用条件的抽象原则；2）通过实例化原则生成任务特定指导，利用反馈与重采样优化候选动作；3）构建执行-抽象反馈循环，持续更新分层记忆。

Result: 在ScienceWorld和ALFWorld任务中实现性能提升；消融实验表明，情境实例化和动作调节对于有效利用原则级知识至关重要。

Conclusion: 通过经验驱动的知识抽象与利用机制，SAGA使LLM代理能够借助累积的抽象知识优化决策，同时保持对执行证据的关联，从而实现持续学习与适应。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in interactive environments, yet their ability to continually evolve from experience remains limited. Although fine-tuning enables adaptation, its dependence on parameter access and high computational costs restrict its flexibility, especially for large-scale and closed-source LLMs. External memory offers an alternative by allowing agents to accumulate experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing methods mainly focus on experience representation and organization, while the acquired knowledge remains tightly coupled with specific tasks and contexts, limiting generalization. A key challenge is how to transform concrete interactions into abstract and reusable knowledge that guides future decisions beyond individual experiences.
  To address this challenge, we propose SAGA (\underline{\textbf{S}}elf-evolving \underline{\textbf{A}}gents through Experience-\underline{\textbf{G}}rounded \underline{\textbf{A}}bstraction), a framework for experience-grounded knowledge abstraction and utilization in LLM agents. SAGA progressively transforms interaction trajectories into episodic descriptions, reusable procedures, and principles with explicit applicability conditions, while maintaining links to execution evidence. Retrieved principles are instantiated into task-specific guidance and used to refine candidate actions through corrective feedback and resampling. This creates an execution--abstraction feedback loop, where accumulated knowledge guides future interactions and new experiences continuously update hierarchical memory. Experiments on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld demonstrate improved task performance, with ablation studies highlighting the importance of contextual instantiation and action regulation for leveraging principle-level knowledge.

</details>


### [51] [Topology-Consistent Task Planning over Cellular Workflow Complexes for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07004)
*Sen Zhao,Jia Tang,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Ding Zou,Xinyu He,Xu Zhang,Junwei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoPlanner 是一个拓扑一致的规划框架，通过将工具依赖图提升为细胞工作流复合体，利用拓扑感知上下文帮助 LLM 代理规划工具序列，特别擅长处理验证-修正循环、分支合并和可重用中间状态等复杂工作流模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器在处理顺序或 DAG 结构时表现良好，但在处理验证-修正循环、分支合并和可重用中间状态等真实世界工具编排中自然出现的工作流模式时面临困难。

Method: TopoPlanner 将工具依赖图提升为细胞工作流复合体，通过 cosheaf 一致的细胞检索获取请求相关的闭合子复合体，对检索到的拓扑进行多维结构推理，并将结果细胞表示与规划器 LLM 接口，用于工具序列生成。

Result: 在四个工具规划基准测试中，针对拓扑引导的循环、合并和循环合并工作流，TopoPlanner 相比提示基线和图增强基线在不同本地 LLM 后端上均取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: TopoPlanner 为处理真实世界复杂工作流模式提供了稳健的解决方案，通过拓扑感知上下文显著改善了 LLM 代理的工具规划能力。

Abstract: Task planning for LLM agents requires workflows that satisfy both user intent and complex sub-task dependencies. While existing planners work well for sequential or directed acyclic graph (DAG)-like structures, they struggle with workflow patterns such as verification-correction loops, convergent branch merging, and reusable intermediate states that arise naturally in real-world tool orchestration. We present TopoPlanner, a topology-consistent planning framework that lifts tool dependency graphs into cellular workflow complexes and uses them as topologyaware context for LLM tool planning. TopoPlanner retrieves a request-relevant closed subcomplex through cosheaf-consistent cellular retrieval, performs multidimensional structural reasoning over the retrieved topology, and interfaces the resulting cellular representation with the planner LLM for tool-sequence generation. Experiments on four tool-planning benchmarks with topology-guided loop, merge, and loop-merge workflows show consistent improvements over prompt-based and graph-enhanced baselines across different local LLM backbones.

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Refusal Patterns: Safe-Role Internalization for Robust and Generalizable LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07023)
*Jinghao Pang,Jitai Hao,Qiang Huang,Zhaochun Ren,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SSRFT通过安全角色内化而非拒绝模式来增强LLM安全性，使用Safe-Role Question-Answer (SRQA)数据集进行监督微调，实现更稳健和可泛化的安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐方法（SFT和RLHF）存在三个主要问题：1）需要大量攻击特定的监督信号和计算资源；2）易受浅层安全对齐影响；3）导致过度拒绝行为。

Method: SSRFT构建Safe-Role Question-Answer (SRQA)数据集，包含心理测量问题、有限黑客攻击提示和安全角色描述。通过角色一致的响应合成、验证和扩展，使模型内化安全价值观和原则而非显式拒绝模式。

Result: 在多个基础和指令模型上，SSRFT比标准SFT实现更稳健和可泛化的安全对齐。具体表现为：1）对预填充攻击具有更强鲁棒性；2）对未见过的黑客攻击领域具有更好泛化能力；3）在良性查询上减少过度拒绝；4）保持模型通用能力。

Conclusion: 安全角色内化是拒绝为中心的安全对齐的有效替代方案，通过内化安全价值观和原则而非依赖显式拒绝模式，实现了更全面和可持续的安全对齐效果。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit harmful or unsafe outputs. Existing safety alignment approaches, including Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), often require substantial attack-specific supervision and computational resources, while remaining susceptible to shallow safety alignment and over-refusal. To address these challenges, we introduce SSRFT(Supervised Safe-Role Fine-Tuning), the first framework that reformulates safety alignment as the internalization of a predefined safe role. SSRFT constructs a Safe-Role Question-Answer (SRQA) dataset from psychometric questions, limited jailbreak prompts, and a safe-role description. Role-consistent responses are synthesized, validated, and expanded into diverse scenarios, enabling models to internalize safety-oriented values and principles rather than explicit refusal patterns. Experiments across multiple Base and Instruct models show that SSRFT achieves more robust and generalizable safety alignment than standard SFT. SSRFT shows substantially greater robustness to prefilling attacks and better generalization to unseen jailbreak domains, while reducing over-refusal on benign queries and preserving the model's general capabilities. These results establish safe-role internalization as an effective alternative to refusal-centric safety alignment. Warning: This paper contains examples of harmful and toxic language.

</details>

*Cristian Martella,Angelo Martella,Antonella Longo,Motaz Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of heterogeneous data sources within the Internet of Things (IoT) across domains such as smart cities, energy management, and environmental monitoring necessitates efficient and scalable data standardization methods. Effective classification of smart data models (SDMs) is essential for facilitating interoperability. However, existing approaches are often limited by high resource consumption and lack applicability in edge environments with constrained computational capabilities. Aiming to bridge this gap, the proposed study evaluates the performance of lightweight open-source language models (LMs) to resolve an input data entity against its corresponding best fitting SDM representation under resource-constrained conditions. It systematically benchmarks a diverse array of models, including general purpose (GP), reasoning-specialized (RS), and code-specialized (CS) architectures, across multiple domain-specific datasets. Addressing the current omission of lightweight, resource-efficient solutions in the literature, the investigation provides significant and valuable insights into model selection, task formulation, and deployment strategies that optimize accuracy and efficiency. A complementary experiment also compares the surveyed large language models (LLMs) against two near-zero-cost similarity baselines (Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) and a lightweight sentence encoder) on the same task, providing a strong reference point for interpreting the practical value of LLM-based classification on edge platforms.

</details>


### [54] [AMBER: Training Long-Horizon Web Agents through Append-Only Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07118)
*Chinmay Savadikar,Zhaoyu Zhang,Mingyu Zhao,Shuang Xie,Han Li,Tianfu Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMBER提出了一种基于追加式记忆的框架，通过让智能体协同学习推理、行动和写入自由形式记忆，并采用追加式规则保证记忆保留，从而解决长时程任务中的上下文预算限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型智能体需要在长时程多步轨迹中维护事实信息、记住执行错误和反馈、跟踪进度，但保持完整历史在上下文中不切实际。现有方法如覆盖式记忆难以学习稀疏结果奖励下的信息保留，且在交互应用中会删除关键信息或环境反馈。

Method: AMBER采用简单可扩展的框架，让智能体协同学习推理、行动和写入自由形式记忆，并使用追加式（append-only）规则保证信息保留，无需重写即可通过端到端强化学习从结果奖励训练，无需大量精心策划的SFT数据。

Result: 在WebArena Lite上，AMBER平均成功率比基于覆盖式记忆的方法提升4.09个百分点，5次重复运行中任务解决比例提升4.8个百分点，且匹配了在更昂贵监督数据上训练的覆盖式基线。AMBER在保持实用token预算的同时，实现了上下文效率、任务性能和可靠长时程执行的强平衡。

Conclusion: AMBER证明了追加式记忆在解决长时程智能体上下文限制问题上的有效性，通过简单规则设计避免了覆盖式记忆的学习难题，在无需大量监督数据的情况下实现了更高的任务成功率。

Abstract: Modern language-model agents increasingly interact with external environments over long-horizon, multi-step trajectories, where the accumulated interaction history can quickly exceed practical context budgets. To ensure reliability, agents must maintain factual information over long horizons, remember execution errors and corrective feedback, and track progress across actions. Several approaches have been proposed to achieve this without the need for maintaining the entire execution history in context, such as using the reasoning and action history, learning to maintain a fixed-size memory through an overwrite mechanism, and periodic summarization. Although overwrite memory can in principle retain anything an append-only memory can, it must learn to carry each fact through every subsequent rewrite, which is difficult to learn from sparse outcome rewards; for interactive applications like web agents, we find that trained overwrite memories delete key information required by the trajectory, as well as corrective feedback received from the environment. We introduce AMBER (Append-only Memory Bank for Evidence Retention) - a simple and scalable framework where an agent jointly learns to reason, act, and write free-form memory, while an append-only rule guarantees retention by construction. This allows AMBER to be trained end-to-end with reinforcement learning from outcome rewards without the need for extensive curated SFT data. On WebArena Lite, AMBER improves average success over overwrite-based memory by 4.09 percentage points, increases the fraction of tasks solved in five repeated runs by 4.8 percentage points, and matches an overwrite baseline trained on substantially more expensive curated supervision. AMBER achieves these improvements while maintaining a practical token budget, providing a strong balance between context efficiency, task performance, and reliable long-horizon execution.

</details>


### [55] [Verifying Coordination in Parallel Coding Agents: NP-Bench and a Scheduling Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07261)
*Sumanyu Muku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种主动规划方法（Nerveplane），通过将多智能体协作编码问题重新表述为调度问题，预先将工作划分为不相交作用域并按生产者-消费者图顺序合并，显著提升了代码集成的成功率和减少冲突，并在NP-Bench基准测试中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多LLM编码智能体团队在并行协作时，即使单个智能体通过测试，合并后常因作用域冲突、契约不匹配导致集成失败；现有反应式协调工具（如冲突检测与警告）在智能体速度下反应太慢，无法有效避免无效编辑。

Method: 将工作分配视为调度问题：基于每个工作项的声明作用域，预先划分互斥的作用域，并沿生产者→消费者依赖图规划合并顺序；将规划器集成到Nerveplane，结合跨会话记忆减少重复错误，并在NP-Bench中评估三种协调模式（无协调、反应式检测、主动规划）。

Result: 主动规划将清洁集成率从1/9提升至9/9，合并冲突从13降至0；在实时破坏性契约变更中，清洁集成率从0提升至前沿模型的1.0和小模型的0.6，且智能体作用域遵守率达100%；跨会话记忆将重复错误率降至0；但将事实路由到智能体未改善长上下文准确率，收益来自工作分配而非模型推理。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作编码的关键在于预先规划工作分配（调度）而非事后反应；Nerveplane的主动规划显著提升集成成功率和冲突解决能力，收益不随模型变强而减弱，且不依赖模型推理能力，证明工作分配机制是提升团队效能的核心。

Abstract: A team of coding agents can look fine agent by agent yet fail as a team: each passes its own tests while the merged result is broken, and single-agent evaluation never catches it. As teams run several LLM coding agents in parallel on one codebase, the agents collide: two rewrite the same function, one codes against a contract a teammate just changed, and integration fails after the work is done. Most coordination tools react (watch for a conflict, then warn), but at agent speed the warning arrives after the wasted edit. We recast the problem as scheduling: take each work item's declared scope, partition the work into disjoint scopes, and order merges along the producer->consumer graph, all up front. We build this planner into Nerveplane and evaluate it with NP-Bench, an environment-grounded three-arm benchmark (no coordination; reactive detection; proactive planning) that verifies integration off a real git merge, both in a deterministic simulation and with live agents. The planner lifts clean-integration from 1/9 to 9/9 scenarios and cuts merge conflicts from 13 to 0, with a gap that grows in the number of agents. On a live breaking contract change it rescues an outcome both baselines miss on every seed: the clean-integration rate rises from 0 (no coordination and reactive detection) to 1.0 on a frontier model and 0.6 on a small one, while agents respect assigned scopes (0/5 leakage). A cross-session memory drops the repeated-mistake rate from 1.00 to 0.00 on strong and weak models alike. We also report a negative result: routing facts to agents does not rescue long-context accuracy at window-fitting scales; its value is cost and capacity, not attention. Across two capability tiers and two vendors, the benefit did not shrink as models got stronger, because it comes from how work is allocated, not model reasoning.

</details>


### [56] [Does the Model Use the Feature? Separating Steering from Mechanism in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07270)
*Tong Che,Yilong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文质疑了将内部特征直接解释为机制的观点，提出两个实证测试（安装和下游恢复）来严格评估特征与行为的关系，发现跟踪概念和引导行为本身不能证明模型使用了该特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部特征通常被解释为机制，因为它们跟踪概念且其操作改变行为。但当通过引导（steering）将特征推到自然范围之外时，其效果可能不再反映模型的计算。需要更严格的实证测试来验证特征-行为关系。

Method: 提出两个测试：1）安装——将表现出行为的输入中的特征值复制到匹配的不表现出行为的输入中；2）下游恢复——在上游编辑后恢复特征值。安装测量特征值是否足以产生行为；移除和下游恢复测量模型使用了多少该特征。对三种表示类型进行测试。

Result: 跟踪概念和引导行为本身不能证明模型使用了该特征。在三个实验中：1）已知实体潜在空间强烈引导知识回避，但安装观察到的值仅转移了很小比例的自然已知-未知回避对比；2）Gemma和Llama中的密集已知-未知方向在安装和移除之间表现出相反的不对称性；3）主体-动词一致特征集如何完全再现和恢复行为取决于如何将值写入模型。

Conclusion: 每个结论仅适用于所测试的干预措施。跟踪一个概念和引导一个行为并不代表模型使用了该特征。特征解释需要谨慎，不能简单推断模型内部处理过程，需要针对特定干预进行验证。

Abstract: Internal features in LLMs are often interpreted as mechanisms when they track a concept and their manipulation changes a related behavior. Yet steering can push a feature far outside its natural range, where its effects need not reflect the model's own computation. We examine this inference and propose an empirical contract whose tests evaluate features at values observed on natural inputs. One test copies a feature's value from an input that shows a behavior into a matched input that does not (installation) or the reverse (removal); the other restores the feature after an upstream edit (downstream rescue). Installation measures how far the feature suffices for the behavior; removal and downstream rescue measure how much the model uses it. Applied to three kinds of representations, the two strengths separate sharply. The published unknown-entity latent strongly steers knowledge abstention, yet installing observed values from either published latent into matched prompts transfers only a small fraction of the natural known--unknown abstention contrast. Dense known--unknown directions show opposite asymmetries between installation and removal in Gemma and Llama, and how fully a released subject--verb agreement feature set reproduces and restores the behavior depends on how its values are written into the model. Tracking a concept and steering a behavior therefore do not by themselves show that the model uses a feature, and each conclusion holds only for the intervention tested.

</details>


### [57] [Understanding and Mitigating Inference-Time Overreliance Using Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07311)
*Luoxi Tang,Yuqiao Meng,Nilesh Auradkar,Muchao Ye,Dazheng Zhang,Zhaohan Xi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM智能体中“记忆过度依赖”的问题，并提出MEMTRIM框架通过在写入时索引记忆证据并在读取时控制其重用，有效缓解了记忆过度依赖现象，同时保留了有用记忆的益处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然智能体记忆允许LLM智能体重用过去的经验，但检索到的记忆即使本身是良性、正确存储且适当检索的，也可能扭曲推理。论文聚焦于这种失败模式——记忆过度依赖。

Method: 提出MEMTRIM框架，这是一个即插即用的系统，在写入时索引记忆证据，在读取时控制其重用。MEMTRIM能够去除重复或冲突的证据，同时保留有用的记忆特异性信息，无需重新训练，适用于基于嵌入和结构化记忆系统。

Result: 通过跨基准测试和记忆架构的实验发现，当过去经验部分转移时，记忆变得具有误导性；MEMTRIM显著减少了记忆过度依赖，同时保持了对有用记忆的益处，适用于多种模型和记忆设置。

Conclusion: 记忆过度依赖在部分查询-记忆重叠时最为严重，MEMTRIM通过智能控制记忆证据的重用有效解决了这一问题，无需重新训练，展示了在LLM智能体记忆系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Agentic memory allows LLM agents to reuse past experience, yet retrieved memories can also distort inference even when they are benign, correctly stored, and appropriately retrieved. We study this failure mode, which we call memory over-reliance. Across benchmarks and memory architectures, we find that memory is useful when past experience transfers to the current task, but can become misleading when only part of the evidence transfers. Failures are strongest under partial query-memory overlap, a pattern further confirmed by controlled experiments thatvary the amount of overlapping evidence. Motivated by this finding, we propose MEMTRIM, a plug-and-play framework that indexes memory evidence at write time and controls its reuse at read time. MEMTRIM removes repeated or conflicting evidence while preserving useful memory-specific information, requires no retraining, and applies to both embedding-based and structured memory systems.Experiments show that MEMTRIM reduces memory overreliance while preserving the benefits of useful memory across models and memory settings.

</details>


### [58] [Rationale-Guided Policy Optimization: Learning to Reason with Adaptive Rationale Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07342)
*Hoang Phan,Minh Pham,Chau Pham,Chinmay Hegde,Trung Le,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RGPO是一种基于ground-truth推理信息引导的强化学习框架，通过将参考解作为临时支架，自适应地利用推理信息来生成改进响应，从而减少奖励稀疏性问题并提升LLM推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy强化学习在提升大语言模型推理能力方面已成为核心范式，但其有效性常受奖励稀疏性限制。现有方法（如使用离策略演示、专家轨迹或模型生成解）通常需要辅助数据与RL任务格式匹配，并依赖从更强模型进行拒绝采样获取训练轨迹，存在数据格式匹配和采样效率问题

Method: RGPO框架自适应地利用ground-truth推理信息，根据模型当前能力动态调整引导策略，同时保持探索自由度。与将参考解作为固定模仿目标不同，RGPO将其作为临时支架：推理信息帮助模型生成改进响应，随后仅将高奖励、模型生成的解转移回原始无引导设置中

Result: 在纯语言和视觉-语言推理设置中，RGPO consistently提升性能优于RLVR基线。消融研究表明自适应推理引导是这些增益的关键贡献者

Conclusion: RGPO提供了一种实用且通用的方法来减少奖励稀疏性、稳定强化学习并改善文本和跨模态模型的推理性能

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning has become a central paradigm for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, its effectiveness is often limited by reward sparsity: when a model fails to discover correct trajectories for difficult problems, the optimization process receives little useful signal and may stagnate. Existing approaches mitigate this issue by incorporating off-policy demonstrations, expert traces, or model-generated solutions, but they typically require the auxiliary data to match the format of the reinforcement-learning task, often relying on rejection sampling from stronger models to obtain suitable training trajectories. We introduce Rationale-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO), a framework that adaptively leverages ground-truth rationale information according to the model's current capability while preserving its freedom to explore. Rather than treating reference solutions as fixed imitation targets, RGPO uses them as temporary scaffolds: rationales help the model generate improved responses, after which only higher-reward, model-generated solutions are transferred back to the original unguided setting. This design allows training to exploit available ground-truth information without requiring off-policy data to follow the same format as the RL task. Across both language-only and vision-language reasoning settings, RGPO consistently improves performance over RLVR baselines, and ablation studies show that adaptive rationale guidance is a key contributor to these gains. These results suggest that RGPO offers a practical and general approach for reducing reward sparsity, stabilizing reinforcement learning, and improving reasoning performance in both text-only and multimodal models.

</details>


### [59] [Evaluating Escalation Signals for LLM Routing: Targets, Controls, and Five Ways to Fool Yourself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07354)
*Ramin Pishehvar,Andrea Morandi,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究使用语义熵作为廉价信号，在小型与大型语言模型间进行查询升级决策，发现语义熵在GSM8K上表现良好（AUROC 0.871），但也揭示早期合成基准结果具有误导性，并提供了评估升级信号的通用检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决定何时将查询从小型语言模型升级到大型语言模型需要一个廉价的预测信号，该信号能在调用大型模型前预测升级是否有帮助。语义熵（最初用于检测幻觉）是候选方案，因为它测量模型采样答案在语义上的不一致程度，高不一致通常表明答案不可靠。

Method: 在三个基准测试和两个模型家族上测试语义熵作为升级信号。通过多种检查方法评估结果：1）将信号与问题难度估计结合；2）比较不同"升级成功"定义的结果；3）检查基准测试是否有足够区分空间；4）评估实时采样的真实成本；5）对重用缓存的替代方案进行预测验证。

Result: 1）在GSM8K上，语义熵能有效区分小型模型的错误（AUROC 0.871），在匹配成本下路由准确率比随机升级提高最多9个百分点；2）早期在合成基准上的强结果被证明是误导性的，简单基于问题难度的规则几乎匹配语义熵；3）实时采样成本可能使路由比直接调用大型模型更昂贵；4）对缓存重用方法的预测模型准确预测了其性能从AUROC 0.908崩溃到0.518（随机水平）。

Conclusion: 论文提供了评估升级信号的通用检查清单：必须验证信号是否添加真实信息而非仅跟踪问题难度；需要考虑不同评估标准；基准测试可能无法提供足够区分空间；必须考虑实际成本；对于缓存重用方法，可以提前预测其在新数据集上的有效性。这些检查可防止误导性结果的报告。

Abstract: Deciding when to escalate a query from a small language model to a larger one requires a cheap signal that predicts, before the large model is called, whether escalating would help. Semantic entropy, originally developed to detect hallucinations, is a natural candidate: it measures how much a model's sampled answers disagree in meaning, and high disagreement often signals an unreliable answer. We test it across three benchmarks and two model families. On GSM8K, with a small/large pair about twelve times apart in size, semantic entropy reliably distinguishes the small model's mistakes (AUROC 0.871) and improves routed accuracy over random escalation by up to nine points at matched cost. An earlier strong-looking result on a synthetic benchmark proved misleading: a simple rule based only on question difficulty, with no model involved, matched semantic entropy almost exactly. This paper's main contribution is a set of checks that catch this before it is reported as real. We show that scoring a cheap, question-only difficulty estimate alongside any signal reveals whether the signal adds real information or just tracks how hard a question looks; that two reasonable definitions of "escalation worked" can produce very different results on the same data; that a benchmark can leave almost no room for any signal to beat simply always using the large model; and that the true cost of live sampling can make routing more expensive than calling the large model directly. For a cheaper alternative that reuses cached past outcomes, we show how to predict whether it will work on a new dataset -- confirmed by correctly forecasting a collapse from AUROC 0.908 to chance level (0.518) ahead of time. We offer these as a general checklist for evaluating escalation signals.

</details>


### [60] [COMPASS: Finding Where Reasoning Lives in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07469)
*Pratyay Dutta,Kowshik Thopalli,Vivek Narayanaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMPASS是一种推理时引导方法，通过识别少数注意力头并沿"正确性"方向引导其激活来提升LLM的数学推理能力，在GSM8K上准确率提升16%，且所需生成标记数比思维链减少20-70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理引导方法需要预定义的推理表征（如思维链提示、对比思维链方向或SAE推导的推理特征）。作者发现对于数学推理，模型自身直接回答尝试的正确性这一简单信号就足以提取推理方向，无需复杂的预定义表征。

Method: 1. 提取表示"正确性"的潜在方向（基于模型直接回答尝试的正确性信号）\n2. 通过logit空间归因分数识别可被有效干预的注意力头\n3. 使用仅每头激活统计数据的轻量级方法，沿正确性方向引导这些头的激活\n4. 干预措施无需重新训练即可转移到未见基准测试

Result: 在三个模型家族和多个数学基准测试上，COMPASS优于激活引导基线：\n- GSM8K准确率平均提升16个百分点\n- 达到思维链准确率，但生成标记数减少20-70%\n- 干预措施可无重新训练地转移到未见基准测试\n- 消融实验表明正确性方向和少数注意力头都是必要条件，效果集中在极少注意力头上

Conclusion: 正确性方向信号和携带该方向的少数注意力头共同构成有效推理引导，效果高度集中在极少数注意力头上，证明了简单信号在推理引导中的有效性。该方法为推理时干预提供了一种更简单、更高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Explicitly eliciting reasoning substantially improves LLM performance. Existing approaches require a predefined characterization of reasoning, whether through CoT prompt design, contrastive CoT directions, or via SAE derived reasoning features. For mathematical reasoning with verifiable answers, we show that a much simpler signal suffices, which is the correctness of the model's own direct answer attempts. This signal yields a latent direction that elicits reasoning. This direction is decodable within the activations of most attention heads, but only a small subset of them can be effectively intervened. We introduce COMPASS, an inference-time steering method that identifies these heads using a logit-space attribution score and steers their activations along the correctness direction, requiring only per-head activation statistics. Across three model families and multiple math benchmarks, COMPASS outperforms the activation-steering baselines we compare against, improves GSM8K accuracy by 16 percentage points on average, and approaches CoT accuracy with 20-70\% fewer generated tokens. Interventions transfer without re-fitting to unseen benchmarks, and ablations show that both the correctness direction and the small set of heads carrying it are necessary, with the effect concentrated in remarkably few heads.

</details>


### [61] [PsyCIDRA: A Dual-Agent Framework for Psychiatric Interviewing and Diagnostic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07473)
*Milad Mohammadi,Fatemeh Akrami Shamsabadi,Zahra Mohseni,Amirhossein Safdarian,Malekfarhad Malek,Hadi Moradi,Hesham Faili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PsyCIDRA是一个双代理框架，将自由形式的精神病学访谈与诊断推理相结合，通过访谈者代理进行引导性对话，诊断推理代理进行证据评估和假设生成，在模拟和盲法研究中表现出优于直接提示的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床推理方面有潜力，但精神病学访谈需要引导不断发展的对话，其进行这种互动评估的能力研究不足。

Method: PsyCIDRA包含两个代理：1) 访谈者代理使用工具维护工作笔记、加载专家技能和检索ICD-11参考来指导调查；2) 诊断推理代理接收访谈记录，报告假设及支持/冲突/缺失的证据，仅在证据充分时提出最终诊断。使用PsyCPG生成的患者档案在模拟中评估。

Result: 在53个评估案例的四种模型上，PsyCIDRA的诊断一致性高于直接提示；在81个保留的模拟案例中，rank-1准确率60.5% vs 51.9%；在101名人类参与的盲法研究中，与心理学家在提出诊断假设上的一致性79.6% vs 直接提示的65.4%。

Conclusion: 研究发现支持LLM代理通过自由形式对话协助精神病学评估的潜力。通过综合考察诊断推理、访谈质量和安全性，这项研究有助于理解精神病学访谈代理的能力和局限性。

Abstract: Large language models show promise in clinical reasoning, but psychiatric interviewing requires guiding an evolving conversation. Their ability to carry out this interactive assessment remains less studied. We present PsyCIDRA, a dual-agent framework linking free-form psychiatric interviewing with diagnostic reasoning for expert review. Its interviewer agent uses tools to maintain working notes, load expert-written skills, and retrieve ICD-11 references to guide inquiry. Its diagnostic reasoning agent then receives the completed interview transcript and reports hypotheses alongside supporting, conflicting, and missing evidence, withholding a final hypothesis when none is sufficiently supported. Using patient profiles generated with PsyCPG, we first evaluate PsyCIDRA in simulation. Across four models on 53 evaluation cases, it achieves higher diagnostic agreement than direct prompting. On 81 held-out simulated cases, rank-1 accuracy is 60.5% versus 51.9%. In a blinded study of 101 human participants in separate arms, PsyCIDRA agrees with psychologists on whether to propose a diagnostic hypothesis in 79.6% of cases, compared with 65.4% for direct prompting. Together, these findings support the potential of LLM agents to assist psychiatric assessment through free-form dialogue. By examining diagnostic reasoning, interview quality, and safety together, this study contributes to understanding the capabilities and limitations of psychiatric interview agents.

</details>

### [63] [Grounding What Shapes the Plan: Rethinking Groundedness for Physical Intelligence in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07521)
*Minkyoung Cho,Zewei Zhou,Wenhao Ding,Shuhan Tan,Boyi Li,Yuxiao Chen,Yan Wang,Zheng Lian,Min-Hung Chen,Chaowei Xiao,Zhuoqing Mao,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Yulong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GroundAct将物理实体作为推理的基本单位，通过轻量级引用令牌保持实体状态的符号化可寻址，实现从推理到动作的显式规划路径，在多种场景中展现出强大的开放循环规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然驾驶模型越来越多地将推理建立在因果关系、空间结构、感知证据和预测未来之上，但存在一个根本性问题：当模型最终输出动作时， groundedness 应该意味着什么？正确 grounded 的推理本身并不能保证理想的驾驶结果。

Method: GroundAct 采用物理实体作为 grounded 的基本单位，通过轻量级引用令牌保持每个选定实体的连续状态可通过符号化推理寻址，仅让被引用实体与不断发展的提案的相互作用来修正计划，建立从推理 grounded 到计划执行的显式路径。

Result: GroundAct 在正常、分布外和安全关键场景中均展现出强大的开放循环规划能力，闭环结果进一步验证了其在模拟驾驶中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于实体作为 grounded 单位并使用引用令牌实现状态寻址的 grounded planning 方法，为确保驾驶模型的推理能够导向理想的驾驶结果提供了实用的框架。

Abstract: Driving models increasingly ground reasoning in causal relations, spatial structure, perceptual evidence, and predicted futures. These advances make reasoning more faithful to the driving scene, but leave a fundamental question unresolved: what should groundedness mean when the model ultimately outputs an action? Correctly grounded reasoning does not, by itself, ensure desirable driving outcomes. We introduce GroundAct, which starts from a simple premise: driving unfolds through physical entities and their interactions. Entities therefore become the unit of grounding; a lightweight reference token keeps each selected entity's continuous state addressable through symbolic reasoning; and only the referenced entities' interactions with the evolving proposal correct the plan. The result is an explicit path from what reasoning grounds to what the plan does, which we call grounded planning. To assess its practical value, we evaluate GroundAct in both open- and closed-loop settings. GroundAct shows strong open-loop planning across normal, out-of-distribution, and safety-critical scenarios, with closed-loop results extending this evidence to driving in simulation.

</details>


### [64] [A Systematic Investigation of Bias in Large Language Models for Advertising Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07544)
*Weiwei Wang,Yinchuan Xu,Jialu Gao,Youkow Homma,Jian Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了大型语言模型在广告相关性判断中的公平性问题，发现模型在判断广告与查询匹配度时受广告主身份、输入语言和人口统计特征影响，存在性别、职业等刻板印象偏见，并提出基于查询相关性和训练数据分布的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）在广告相关性判断中的应用日益广泛，其判断的公平性问题却鲜受关注。现有研究缺乏对LLM在广告相关性判断中公平性的系统性评估，特别是在广告主身份、输入语言和人口统计特征等因素对判断结果影响方面的研究不足。

Method: 采用反事实框架系统评估LLM在广告相关性判断中的公平性问题。实验使用GPT-4o作为分类相关性判断器，以及专门训练用于相关性预测的Qwen-7B模型。通过真实广告日志中采样的查询-广告对研究广告主身份和语言影响，使用受控合成查询研究就业、住房、信贷等领域的人口统计特征关联。

Result: 实验发现改变广告主身份或输入语言会显著影响相关性评估结果；人口统计比较显示出与常见刻板印象一致的模式，特别是涉及性别和职业的比较；缓解策略的效果取决于广告主信息是否与查询相关以及训练数据中广告主标签的分布情况。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLM广告相关性判断中存在的公平性风险，为广告从业者识别这些风险和开发合适的缓解方法提供了重要指导，同时证明了缓解策略的有效性依赖于查询相关性和数据分布特征。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to judge how well an advertisement matches a query, but the fairness of these judgments has received limited attention. We conduct a systematic study of fairness in relevance judgments made by LLMs for queries and advertisements. Our counterfactual framework examines the effects of advertiser identity and possible popularity, input language, and demographic wording. We study GPT-4o as a categorical relevance judge and a Qwen-7B model trained specifically for relevance prediction. The advertiser and language experiments use query and advertisement pairs sampled from real advertising logs. Controlled synthetic queries are used to study demographic associations in employment, housing, and credit. For both models, changing the advertiser identity or input language can alter the relevance assessment. Selected demographic comparisons also show patterns consistent with common stereotypes, particularly those involving gender and occupation. We further study mitigation during model inference and training. The results indicate that its effectiveness depends on whether advertiser information is relevant to the query and how advertiser labels are distributed in the training data. These findings can help advertising practitioners identify fairness risks and develop suitable mitigation methods for LLM relevance systems.

</details>


### [65] [Decoupled Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07556)
*Xinle Wu,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeOrch提出了一种分离任务规划与工人选择的新方法，通过两阶段规划器和轻量级匹配器提高多智能体系统的可迁移性和信用分配效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式编排方法将规划与固定工人池耦合，且从同一终端结果训练分解与协作，导致可迁移性差且信用分配不清晰

Method: DeOrch采用两阶段规划器：先无工人信息地分解任务，再使用无工人身份的匹配反馈选择协作操作；通过轻量级匹配器从固定探测集估计工人适应性，并利用上下文老虎机在线自适应

Result: 在多种任务上，DeOrch比现有方法调用更少工人，可迁移到全新工人池且无需重新训练，两个组件均带来持续提升

Conclusion: 将规划与工人选择解耦显著增强了多智能体系统的可迁移性和信用分配能力，使编排更高效、有效

Abstract: Learned orchestration can automatically construct effective language-model multi-agent systems, but existing approaches couple planning to fixed worker pools and train decomposition and collaboration from the same terminal outcome, limiting transfer and obscuring credit assignment. We introduce DeOrch, which separates worker-agnostic planning from concrete worker selection. Its two-stage planner first decomposes the task without worker information, then chooses collaboration operations using compact, worker-identity-free matchability feedback from the pool, enabling conditional credit assignment to decomposition and collaboration decisions. A lightweight matcher estimates worker suitability from behavior on a fixed probe set and adapts online with a contextual bandit, allowing new workers to be incorporated without retraining the planner or matcher. Across diverse in- and out-of-distribution tasks, DeOrch outperforms prior automatic MAS orchestration methods with fewer worker calls than competing learned orchestrators, remains effective when transferred to an entirely unseen worker pool without retraining, and shows consistent gains from both components.

</details>


### [66] [LOGIC: An LLM Benchmark for Intent-Grounded Change Impact in Aerospace Electrical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07580)
*Muhammad Faraz Shoaib,Muhammad Qasim,Raisulhaq Mohammed Rizwan,Rahmatullah Safdar,Muzammil Adnan Shaik,Abdul Aleem Mohammed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LOGIC是一种针对航空航天电气设计变更评估的受控基准测试框架，通过将候选变更选择与变更传播分离，区分选择错误与传播错误。该框架包含168个场景（144个选择案例和24个放弃案例），在96个明确锚定的选择案例中，仅结构证据的候选F1达到1.0000，而24个关系改写案例中，仅结构证据F1为0.0000。研究建议在无法可靠确定意图时，结合字面证据、语言模型推理和工程审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空航天电气设计修订包含多个真实变更，但工程请求可能仅授权其中一部分。传播所有检测到的差异会产生过于广泛的影响报告。需要一种能够区分候选选择错误与下游传播错误的方法。

Method: LOGIC框架将请求接地到确定性候选变更清单，然后通过类型化的电气可追溯图传播选定的变更。采用三种7-8B模型评估，对比意图无关、词汇和结构证据方法。包含168个场景（144个选择案例，24个放弃案例）。采用严格证据门控和证据为空的放弃策略。

Result: 在96个明确锚定的选择案例中，仅结构证据F1达到1.0000，词汇匹配为0.9677；在12个关系改写案例中，词汇F1为0.1772，结构证据F1为0.0000，大型语言模型为0.5000-0.6400。模型接地效果随候选清单从4增加到64个变更而下降，但受影响元素和类型路径准确率保持稳定。严格证据门控抑制假阳性但可能移除正确语义选择。放弃策略将严格放弃准确率提高到0.6667，不安全报告率降至0.1667，但可回答案例覆盖率下降16.0-27.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 当无法可靠确定意图时，应结合字面证据、语言模型推理和工程审查。四个冲突请求对每个模型仍然不安全。

Abstract: Aerospace electrical-design revisions can contain multiple genuine changes, although an engineering request may authorize only a subset. Propagating every detected difference can therefore produce overly broad impact reports. We present LOGIC, a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework in which locally deployable language models ground a request in a deterministic candidate-change inventory before selected changes are propagated through a typed electrical traceability graph. This separation permits candidate-selection errors to be distinguished from downstream propagation errors. LOGIC contains 168 scenarios, including 144 selection and 24 abstention cases. We evaluate three 7--8B models against intent-agnostic, lexical, and structured-evidence methods, with an oracle-root upper bound. On 96 explicitly anchored selection cases, gate-only structured evidence achieves candidate F1 of 1.0000, compared with 0.9677 for token-lexical matching. On 12 relational-paraphrase cases, token-lexical F1 is 0.1772 and gate-only F1 is 0.0000, compared with 0.5000--0.6400 for the large language models. Model grounding degrades as candidate inventories grow from 4 to 64 changes, while affected-element and typed-path accuracy remain comparatively stable when frozen selections are replayed over graphs of approximately 1K to 100K nodes. Strict evidence gating suppresses false positives but can remove correct semantic selections. An exploratory evidence-empty abstention policy raises strict abstention accuracy to 0.6667 for all three models and reduces unsafe-report rates to 0.1667, while decreasing answerable-case coverage by 16.0--27.1 percentage points. Four of six conflicting requests remain unsafe for each model. These findings support combining literal evidence and language-model reasoning with engineering review when intent cannot be established reliably.

</details>


### [67] [Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation with Cross-Platform User History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07588)
*Yu Xia,Jiangfan Zhang,Jun Xiao,Julian McAuley,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了个人代理中介推荐范式，通过平台本地信息和跨平台历史记录的协同作用来优化推荐结果。研究开发了MediateRec基准测试和PAMO优化算法，证明了算法的理论最优性，并验证了个人代理在推荐系统中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统正从以平台为中心的个人化转向由用户主导的个人化，其中个人LLM代理可以在不同服务间代表用户行动。然而，平台排名可能编码了个人代理无法观察到的强烈人群证据，因此需要平衡有益的挽救与有害的覆盖。

Method: 1. 提出个人代理中介推荐（PAMR）范式；2. 开发MediateRec基准测试，包含可扩展的代理跨平台环境和受控的平台-代理信息边界；3. 提出个人归因中介优化（PAMO）算法，通过反事实遮蔽跨平台历史来估计个人中介支持，并在平台相对价值下限下重新分配排名感知的优势质量。

Result: 实验表明个人代理中介能够实现有意义的平台修正，但即使强大的专有LLM也会引入不可忽视的有害覆盖。PAMO在所见和未见的目标平台以及真实跨平台测试中均优于匹配的结果导向强化学习，并实现了更好的挽救-损害平衡。

Conclusion: PAMO证明了个人代理中介推荐范式在推荐系统中的有效性，通过理论证明了算法的局部最优性，并解决了平台信息不对称带来的挑战。

Abstract: Modern recommendation is shifting from platform-centric personalization toward user-governed personalization, where a personal LLM agent can act on the user's behalf across services. We formalize this emerging paradigm as Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation: a platform recommender ranks a candidate set using platform-local information, and a personal agent uses user-authorized cross-platform history to mediate the resulting ranking and produce the final top-K slate. Such mediation is nontrivial: the platform ranking can encode strong population evidence that the personal agent cannot observe, so effective mediation must therefore balance beneficial rescues against harmful overrides. To study this trade-off, we introduce MediateRec, a benchmark that includes scalable proxy cross-platform environments and a real cross-platform test under a controlled platform-agent information boundary. To train the agent to use cross-platform history effectively, we further propose Personal Attribution Mediation Optimization (PAMO), which counterfactually masks that history to estimate personal mediation support and reallocates rank-aware advantage mass under a platform-relative value floor. We theoretically prove that PAMO preserves cutoff-level advantage mass and is locally optimal among first-order reallocations that preserve this mass without lowering average platform-relative value. Experiments on MediateRec show that personal-agent mediation enables meaningful platform corrections, yet even strong proprietary LLMs introduce non-negligible harmful overrides. PAMO consistently improves over matched outcome-only RL across seen and unseen target platforms and on the real cross-platform test, while achieving a better rescue-harm balance.

</details>


### [68] [VALSE: Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficient Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07606)
*Jia-Dong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了垂直自适应层跳过的理论框架，提出了VALSE方法，并证明了三个基础结果：计算成本公式、跳层模型与全层函数空间的关系、以及VALSE与混合专家架构的结构对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了建立垂直自适应层跳过的理论框架，解决模型深度效率问题，探索如何通过按样本选择性跳过冗余层来提升计算效率。

Method: 提出VALSE（Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficiency）方法：采用轻量级难度估计器对输入进行评分，通过逐层门控选择性跳过冗余层（包括任意中间层），实现非连续的按样本层跳过。

Result: 证明三个基础定理：1）定理2给出任意样本跳过计划的期望FLOPs公式；2）定理10和11证明跳层模型严格包含于但有意义地近似全层函数空间；3）命题6建立VALSE与混合专家架构的结构对偶性。

Conclusion: VALSE作为按样本非连续层跳过方法，能够为每个输入仅激活必要的深度，初步验证了其可行性，为提升深度神经网络计算效率提供了新的理论依据和实践方案。

Abstract: This paper establishes a theoretical framework for vertical adaptive layer skipping, proving three foundational results: (i) an Expected FLOPs formula (theorem 2) giving a closed-form expression for the computational cost of arbitrary per-sample skip schedules as a function of layer-wise skip probabilities; (ii) function-space superset (theorem 10) and strict inclusion (theorem 11) theorems showing that skip-layer models are strictly contained in---yet meaningfully approximate---the full-layer function space, with an explicit separating example; and (iii) a structural duality between VALSE and Mixture-of-Experts architectures (proposition 6), positioning vertical depth-wise sparsity as the orthogonal counterpart to horizontal width-wise sparsity. Building on this theory, we propose VALSE (Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficiency), a per-sample, non-contiguous layer skipping method: a lightweight difficulty estimator scores each input from the first few layers, and per-layer gates selectively skip redundant layers---including arbitrary middle layers while retaining deeper ones---so that only the necessary depth is activated for each input, whose feasibility is preliminarily assessed at prototype scale.

</details>


### [69] [Massive Activation Gating Channel in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07661)
*Minjia Mao,Shi Chen,Bowen Yin,Xiao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLMs中大规模激活现象由输入嵌入中的单一"大规模激活门控通道"(MAGC)控制，该通道位置固定；当MAGC值足够大(或小)时，spike FFN输出呈现大规模激活，其机制为输出渐近退化为混合少数down-projection矩阵列的二次形式，这些列呈现大规模激活形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模激活是LLMs中普遍存在的现象，但token在通过预训练LLM传播时如何产生大规模激活的机制尚未被充分理解。

Method: 通过分析6个跨4个模型家族和不同模型规模的LLM，识别出输入嵌入中一个固定位置的门控通道——大规模激活门控通道(MAGC)，并验证其存在与效果；从理论上解释MAGC诱导大规模激活的机制。

Result: 验证了MAGC的存在和效果：当MAGC值足够大(或小)时，spike FFN输出呈现大规模激活。理论上，当MAGC值足够大(或小)时，spike FFN输出渐近退化为二次形式，混合down-projection矩阵的少数列，这些列呈现大规模激活形状，因此输出呈现大规模激活。

Conclusion: 大规模激活的出现由MAGC控制；其机制是MAGC值足够大(或小)时，spike FFN输出渐近退化为混合特定down-projection矩阵列的二次形式，这些列呈现大规模激活形状，从而产生大规模激活。

Abstract: Massive activations, a phenomenon in which a small number of hidden channels exhibit exceptionally large magnitudes, are pervasive in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanism by which a token develops massive activations as it propagates through a pretrained LLM remains poorly understood. In this paper, we find that the emergence of massive activations is controlled by a single channel in the input embedding to a spike feed-forward network (FFN). The position of this channel is fixed for a particular LLM. We name this channel the massive activation gating channel (MAGC). When the value of the MAGC is sufficiently large (or small, depending on the LLM), the output of the spike FFN exhibits massive activations. Examining six LLMs across four model families and different model sizes, we verify the existence and effect of MAGC. We further provide a theoretical explanation of the mechanism by which MAGC induces massive activations. When the value of MAGC is sufficiently large (or small), the output of a spike FFN asymptotically reduces to a quadratic form that mixes a few columns of the down-projection matrix of the FFN. Since these columns exhibit the shape of massive activations, the output therefore exhibits massive activations.

</details>


### [70] [Evidence Before Sampling: Interpretable Implicit Negative Candidate Discovery for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07708)
*Shreya Rajpal,Sonia Sharma,Swapnil Parekh,Lisa Li,Jeyendran Balakrishnan,Nagaraj Janardhana,Andrew Mattarella-Micke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据的负样本候选发现方法，通过符号化规则和LLM解释，在稀疏、偏斜的真实推荐场景中实现可解释且符合业务目标的负样本选择，提升了推荐系统的下游性能和模型训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统缺乏显式负反馈，且常用负采样方法将未观测到的交互直接视为负样本，但缺失交互并不代表用户不感兴趣。特别是在商业推荐场景中，负信号需要可解释并符合业务目标，因此需要更可靠的负样本发现机制。

Method: 1) 通过隐式负候选发现识别由观测客户行为支持的非观测交互；2) 将这些模式编码为符号规则；3) 基于支持度、信息量和产品相关性对规则进行评分；4) 按证据对保留规则进行排序；5) 利用LLM结合业务目标和领域知识对保留规则进行解释；6) 将统计证据与解释结合生成最终报告。

Result: 在工业B2B环境和五个公开数据集上的评估表明，该方法在候选质量评估中精度高于基线，且在工业任务中，与随机选择（每正例4负例）相比，下游测试PR-AUC提升12.5%。研究结果证实负候选的有效性可以独立于下游推荐性能进行评估。

Conclusion: 区分负候选有效性与下游推荐性能的能力，使得基于证据、业务对齐且可解释的负样本选择成为可能，从而在稀疏、偏斜的真实推荐场景中同时提升可解释性和模型训练效果。

Abstract: Recommender systems learn from observed user-item interactions, but explicit negative feedback is often unavailable. Since deep learning models require negative signals for training, negative sampling methods typically treat selected unobserved interactions as negatives. However, a missing interaction does not explain why a user is uninterested in an item or whether there is sufficient evidence to label it negative.
