This is especially important in business recommendation, where negative signals should be interpretable and aligned with business objectives. We formulate implicit negative candidate discovery to identify unobserved interactions supported by observed customer behavior. We encode these patterns as symbolic rules, score them based on support, informativeness, and product relevance, and rank the retained rules by evidence. An LLM then interprets the retained rules using business objectives and domain knowledge; the interpretations are combined with the statistical evidence in the final report. We evaluate our method in an industrial B2B setting and across five public recommendation datasets. Candidate-quality evaluations in the industrial setting and three public datasets show higher precision than the evaluated baselines, while symbolic selection improves downstream test PR-AUC by 12.5% over random selection with four negatives per positive example in the industrial task. Our results show that negative candidate validity can be evaluated separately from downstream recommendation performance. This distinction enables evidence-based, business-aligned, and explainable negative selection, improving both interpretability and model training in sparse, skewed, real-world recommendation settings.

</details>


### [71] [How Well Do LLMs Reason with Noisy Evidence? An Active Visual Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07751)
*Bach Nguyen,Zhaonan Li,Mau Son Nguyen,Sanika Chavan,Nilay Kumar,Hong Anh Nguyen,Khoa Vo,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VisualNoiseQA是一个新的基准测试，研究文本LLM在有噪声视觉反馈（通过自一致性暴露不确定性）时如何进行主动推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界推理需要主动收集信息，但现有基准要么假设反馈可信，要么引入噪声但不提供明确的不确定性信号。需要研究当证据本身不确定时LLM应该如何推理。

Method: 构建自动化的基准：文本LLM迭代查询两个噪声VLM，通过自一致性暴露经验不确定性信号。只保留传感器不一致但人类可解的问题。评估涵盖感知、图表理解和知识密集型推理（1000个实例）。

Result: 在1000个实例上评估了多个LLM推理者，展示了不同LLM如何利用不确定性信号进行鲁棒推理。

Conclusion: VisualNoiseQA提供了受控环境来研究LLM如何利用不确定性信号进行鲁棒推理，填补了现有基准的空白。

Abstract: Real-world reasoning rarely reduces to static question answering: agents must actively gather information from tools and sensors that are often noisy and unreliable. Yet most existing active reasoning benchmarks assume that environmental feedback is trustworthy, or introduce noise without exposing an explicit, calibrated uncertainty signal, leaving open how LLMs should reason when the evidence itself is uncertain. We introduce VisualNoiseQA, a novel benchmark for active reasoning under noisy visual feedback. A text-only LLM must solve VQA problems by iteratively querying a fixed, off-the-shelf VLM treated as a stochastic visual sensor. For each query, we draw multiple samples and expose an empirical uncertainty signal via self-consistency, enabling the reasoner to probe from different angles and decide what to ask next and when to stop. Our construction is automatic and scalable: starting from diverse VQA sources and two noisy VLMs, we retain only questions where the sensor is inconsistent yet human-solvable. We evaluate multiple LLM reasoners on 1,000 instances spanning perception, chart understanding, and knowledge-intensive reasoning. VisualNoiseQA thus provides a controlled playground to study how different LLMs exploit uncertainty signals for robust reasoning.

</details>


### [72] [ST-Bench: A Spatial-Temporal Benchmark for Multi-Agent System Generation on Scientific Research Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07763)
*Qi Cheng,Rongchao Dong,Shengyu Chen,Licheng Liu,Dan Lu,Zhengzhang Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Haifeng Chen,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了ST-Bench基准测试，用于评估多智能体系统（MAS）在复杂科学数据分析任务上相对于单智能体的性能和成本优势。研究发现MAS在大多数情况下优于单智能体，但收益是有条件的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然多智能体系统（MAS）在编码、数学和问答任务上表现出优于单智能体的能力，但其是否在复杂科学数据分析任务中也具有优势仍不明确。研究旨在验证MAS在科学数据分析中的性能，并量化其优势程度和额外成本。

Method: 1. 创建ST-Bench基准测试，包含100个源自地球科学（水文学、农业、湿地甲烷研究）的数据科学任务，扩展为2,067个经过领域专家验证的查询。2. 评估5种MAS方法和2种训练协议，与单智能体基线在相同的GPT-5模型上进行比较。

Result: 1. 10个MAS配置中有9个超过最便宜的单智能体基线，最强配置达到单智能体基线综合得分的近3倍。2. 优势主要来自覆盖范围：训练好的工作流在更大比例的查询上产生现实的数值指标。3. 在条件输出质量上，MAS与单智能体相当。4. 最强MAS配置需要约4倍的单智能体推理时间，但更经济的MAS工作流可在不到2倍成本下捕获大部分收益。

Conclusion: MAS在科学数据分析中确实能提供可衡量的好处，但这种好处是有条件的而非普遍的。MAS专业化能带来性能提升，但需要权衡成本和收益。

Abstract: The rapid progress of LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) has shown that they largely outperform single agents on coding, math, and QA tasks, where executable tests provide a binary success signal. Whether this advantage transfers to real scientific data analysis remains untested. We introduce ST-Bench, a benchmark designed to answer two questions: whether MAS outperform single agents on complex scientific data analysis tasks, and if so, by how much and at what additional cost. ST-Bench contains 100 data science tasks adapted from published Earth science studies across hydrology, agriculture, and wetland methane research, expanded into 2,067 queries grounded in additional published studies and validated by domain experts. Using ST-Bench, we evaluate five recent MAS generation methods under two training protocols, against single-agent baselines on the same GPT-5 backbone. Nine of the ten MAS configurations exceed the cheapest single-agent baseline, with the strongest reaching nearly three times its composite score. This gain is primarily attributable to coverage: trained workflows produce realistic numerical metrics on a larger fraction of queries, while the quality of those metrics, conditional on producing realistic output, is comparable to that of the single-agent baseline. The strongest configuration requires approximately four times the single-agent inference time, whereas a more economical workflow captures the majority of the benefit at less than twice the cost. MAS specialization confers measurable benefit on scientific data analysis, but the benefit is conditional rather than universal.

</details>


### [73] [OTel: Open Telco AI Datasets, Benchmarks, and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07766)
*Farbod Tavakkoli,Gregory Diamos,Kenneth Church,David Kanter,Mark Austin,Imtiaz Karim,Mirza Masfiqur Rahman,Merouane Abdelkader Debbah,Zeinab Nezami,Ali Maatouk,Leandros Tassiulas,Rex Ying,Nick Sorros,Louis Powell,Nikolaos Vasiloglou,Ashish Vaswani,Somanshu Singla,Adarsh Chaluvaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Open Telco (OTel)是一个开放的电信AI资源平台，提供电信数据集、30个全参数微调基线模型（10个嵌入模型、3个重排序器、17个语言模型），支持检索、重排序、指令调优和安全/回避任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电信领域缺乏开放数据集和基准的问题，为检索、重排序、指令调优和安全/回避等AI任务提供统一的数据资源和评估基准。

Method: 构建OTel资源，包括：1）文档化的电信数据源；2）保留的评估分区；3）基于开放权重模型的微调训练方案；4）训练好的嵌入模型、重排序器和上下文感知语言模型；5）安全/回避数据；6）30个全参数微调基线模型（10个嵌入模型、3个重排序器、17个语言模型）。

Result: 1）资源已获得广泛使用：模型下载超1600万次，获得157+篇媒体报道；2）性能提升显著：嵌入检索达93.1% NDCG@10，重排序达0.947 MRR@10，语言模型正确率达87.8%。

Conclusion: OTel为电信AI研究提供了一个可复现的起点，邀请社区扩展数据、改进嵌入和重排序模型，构建更强的上下文感知电信LLM。

Abstract: We present Open Telco (OTel), an open telecom AI resource that releases derived telecom datasets for retrieval, reranking, instruction tuning, and safety/abstention, together with 30 full-parameter post-trained baselines spanning 10 embedding models, 3 rerankers, and 17 language models. The community has already engaged substantially with the resource: as of May 3, 2026, the released models have been downloaded over 16 million times and the project has received 157+ pieces of media coverage worldwide. Building on prior open telecom datasets and benchmarks, OTel provides documented telecom data sources, held-out evaluation partitions, trained embedding models, rerankers, context-grounded LLMs, and safety/abstention data in one unified resource. Each baseline starts from an open-weight model and is post-trained on OTel-derived data using an open training recipe, then evaluated on held-out OTel evaluation partitions. OTel post-training improves performance across all three model families: embedding retrieval reaches 93.1% NDCG@10, reranking reaches 0.947 MRR@10, and language-model correctness reaches 87.8%. We release OTel as a reproducible starting point and invite the community to expand the data, improve embedding and reranking models, and build stronger context-grounded telecom LLMs.

</details>


### [74] [Persistent Memory in Multi-Agent LLM Inference: What It Costs, What It Buys, and When You Can Tell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07782)
*Hochan Son,Kyungdoe Han,Jaehan Koh,Xiaowu Dai,Wenlu Xu,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估在代理系统中添加持久层存储推理轨迹对准确性和KV缓存内存的影响，发现持久层无准确率提升但能减少峰值缓存


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对长上下文推理中KV缓存内存受限的问题，质疑持久层存储推理轨迹的实际价值，质疑单问题基准测试中检索功能的必要性

Method: 在三层代理架构上测量KV缓存大小和准确率，对比分解推理方法与单次通过和检索增强基线，在8组数据集对上各100次迭代进行控制实验

Result: 分解推理将峰值KV工作集从35.5-35.3 MiB降至14.3 MiB，但持久层增加0.368 MiB峰值缓存且无可检测的准确率变化（95%置信区间[-0.011, +0.046]）

Conclusion: 提出代理记忆消融研究必须满足的条件和检测程序，认为单问题基准测试的结构性特征导致检索功能无信息量，错误地夸大或制造了效果

Abstract: Decomposing long-context inference across cooperating agents bounds the active KV cache per call rather than total evidence, which matters when KV-cache memory binds. Many such systems add a persistent tier storing and recalling reasoning traces, usually validated by an ablation reporting an accuracy gain. We measure both on one three-tier agent architecture. Decomposition delivers: peak KV working set of 14.3 MiB per query against 35.5 and 35.3 MiB for single-pass and retrieval-augmented baselines. The persistent tier does not: across eight controlled dataset pairs at n=100 per arm it costs +0.368 MiB [+0.167, +0.590] of peak cache and produces no detectable accuracy change (+0.015, 95% CI [-0.011, +0.046]). We argue the null is structural: single-question benchmarks supply each item with its own evidence and score it independently, and correctness requires resetting stored traces between conditions, so recall has nothing informative to retrieve. Reaching it took four measurement corrections -- three inflating the apparent benefit, the fourth making an effect that size look resolvable -- none visible in the results table. We give the conditions an agent-memory ablation must satisfy and detection procedures that need no knowledge of the specific defect.

</details>


### [75] [OOPMAS: Object-Oriented Multi-Agent Systems for Query-Level Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07787)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OOPMAS是一个训练无关的框架，能在查询级别动态生成智能体集合和协调工作流，通过面向对象表示和动态技能库实现无需微调的上下文改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS设计方法主要在任务层面操作，生成单一固定工作流，无法适应查询难度差异大和任务类型异构的现实工作负载条件。

Method: 将智能体表示为带有角色、工具和持久状态的面向对象类定义，工作流表示为对这些智能体对象的可执行主函数，通过动态技能库在优化轮次中累积执行反馈，实现无需梯度更新或微调的上下文改进。

Result: 在涵盖代码、数学和问答的混合任务基准上，OOPMAS达到89.6%准确率，比最强基线高出18.1个百分点；跨四个LLM模型的研究显示一致性扩展，最强模型达到92.4%准确率。

Conclusion: OOPMAS通过查询级工作流生成和动态技能学习有效处理异构任务和变化查询难度，展现了在多样化工作负载下的强大性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by large language models have shown strong performance across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and question answering. However, existing methods for automating MAS design mostly operate at the task level, producing a single fixed workflow per benchmark that is applied uniformly to all queries. This assumption fails under realistic conditions. Query difficulty varies widely within a task, and real-world workloads mix heterogeneous task types. We introduce OOPMAS, a training-free framework that generates both the agent set and the coordination workflow at the granularity of individual queries. Agents are represented as object-oriented class definitions with dedicated roles, tools, and persistent state, and workflows are expressed as executable main functions over these agent objects. A dynamic skill library accumulates structured lessons from execution feedback across optimization rounds, enabling in-context improvement without any gradient updates or fine-tuning. On a mixed-task benchmark of queries spanning code, math, and QA, OOPMAS achieves 89.6% accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 18.1 percentage points. A model-swap study across four LLM backbones shows consistent scaling, reaching 92.4% with the strongest model.

</details>


### [76] [Illusory Pattern Perception Drives Spurious Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07791)
*Peihua Mai,Zhuoyan Shao,Xinbao Qiao,Meng Zhang,Xinyue Zhou,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文首次系统研究LLM的“错觉模式感知”现象，发现LLM比人类更容易产生从随机数据中推断有意义的模式的倾向，这种认知错觉可能影响LLM推理的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错觉模式感知是人类常见的认知倾向，LLM可能也存在这种倾向并导致系统性推理错误。这是首次对LLM中错觉模式感知的系统性研究，通过经典心理范式验证LLM是否具备类似人类的心理错觉。

Method: 1. 将经典心理范式改编为三个任务，与人类行为直接比较；2. 开发基于稀疏自编码器(SAE)的特征可解释性框架，分析LLM内部表示；3. 通过实证对比揭示LLM错觉模式感知的表现形式与机制。

Result: 1. LLM比人类表现出更强的错觉模式感知；2. 模型倾向于过度将频繁正向属性与多数群体/大型组织关联；3. 倾向于从模糊事件中构建因果叙事；4. 整体频率感知和分析认知取向与错觉感知的出现相关。

Conclusion: LLM存在一种以前未被充分探索的认知类错觉，可能影响其推理可靠性。该发现揭示了LLM潜在的认知偏差，为提升LLM可靠性提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Illusory pattern perception is a well-documented human cognitive tendency to infer meaningful relationships in data that is actually random. Such a tendency, often described as "connecting the dots" where none exist, can result in systematic reasoning errors. This paper investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit such perceptual tendencies, which can lead to systematic errors in downstream applications. To our knowledge, this work presents the first systematic study of illusory pattern perception in LLMs, adapting classic psychological paradigms to three tasks with direct empirical comparison to human behaviors. We find that LLMs frequently exhibit stronger illusory pattern perception than humans. In particular, models tend to over-associate frequent positive attributes with majority groups or large organizations, and show increased tendencies to construct causal narratives from ambiguous events. To uncover the mechanism behind these behaviors, we develop a feature interpretability framework based on Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to analyze internal representations. Our results reveal that holistic frequency perception and analytic cognitive orientation are linked to the emergence of illusory perceptions. These findings highlight a previously underexplored cognitive-like illusion that may affect the reliability of LLM reasoning. Code available at https://github.com/NusIoraPrivacy/illusory.

</details>


### [77] [Thin Evidence, Thick Priors: How Language Models Substitute Identity for Missing Financial Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07798)
*Saanvi Khetan,Sankar Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究大语言模型在缺乏具体财务信息时，如何根据用户身份差异（如性别、家庭规模）给出不同的投资建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们越来越多地向大语言模型询问财务建议，但很少提供完整的财务信息，论文关注模型如何处理这种信息缺口。

Method: 保持财务状况不变，只改变投资者身份；测试从8个事实到0个事实的披露条件；使用96,600个提示词测试Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct；采用双路聚类bootstrap和消融实验分析网络内部机制。

Result: 两个身份相同但财务信息不同的用户，建议差异从4.78个百分点（完全披露）增加到10.34个百分点（无披露），比例达2.16倍；身份因素在完全披露时仅解释5%的变异性，无披露时解释96%；家庭规模是唯一随证据减少而影响增大的属性；消除性别方向后整体身份影响仍保持不变。

Conclusion: 基于这些模型的顾问系统需要根据用户实际披露水平进行审计，并从整体身份角度进行评估，而非逐个属性分析。

Abstract: People increasingly ask large language models what to do with their money, yet seldom describe their finances in full. This paper asks what a model does with the gap. Holding finances fixed and changing only who the investor is said to be, we grade the financial evidence in the prompt from eight facts to none and measure how far the recommended equity allocation moves. Across 96,600 prompts to Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, built from 100 financial profiles, 138 personas and seven disclosure conditions, the average gap between two personas with identical finances rises from 4.78 percentage points at full disclosure to 10.34 points with no financial facts. A two-way cluster bootstrap counting duplicated prompts once places the ratio at 2.16 (95% interval 1.69 to 2.79), and the rise is already 1.69-fold with a single fact left. Identity explains 5% of within-profile variation in advice at full disclosure and 96% with no disclosure. Household size is the only attribute whose influence grows reliably as evidence is withdrawn. Once standard errors are clustered on the persona, the unit to which identity was assigned, most attribute-specific interactions reported in the conference version lose significance, and gender instead appears as a small standing gap that full disclosure does not close. Stating risk appetite alone brings the swing into the range seen with two to seven generic facts. With no facts, the model's one-line rationale cites incomes, debts and savings it was never told, and these invented finances turn adverse more often for larger households. Inside the network, gender is linearly decodable at every layer, and ablating the gender direction at five layers leaves the aggregate identity swing unchanged. Advisory systems built on such models should be audited at the disclosure levels users actually reach, and judged across the whole identity space rather than one attribute at a time.

</details>


### [78] [DHCG: Dynamic Construction of Hierarchical Collaboration Graphs for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07835)
*Jie Ren,Jiakang Yuan,Chenyu Huang,Hezeer Ma,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DHCG框架将MAS设计重新框架化为POMDP，通过动态分层协作图和动作感知偏好优化，实现了智能体组成和规模的自适应调整，在代码生成、数学推理等领域达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS方法存在组成有限、依赖关系错位和规模不灵活等问题，限制了执行过程中适应推理需求的能力。

Method: 1. 将MAS设计重新框架化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）；2. 提出DHCG框架，包含Planner、Worker、Generator三个模块；3. Planner根据反馈生成互补角色并路由信息，动态构建分层协作图；4. 引入动作感知偏好优化训练Planner。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和领域特定推理基准上达到SOTA平均性能，比单智能体基线提高13.06分，比静态和动态MAS基线高2.77-8.02分；证明了跨不同Planner骨干和未见Worker模型的一般化能力。

Conclusion: DHCG通过动态智能体编排和分层协作图构建，成功解决了现有MAS在组成灵活性、依赖关系和规模自适应方面的局限，为复杂问题求解提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated strong capabilities in solving complex problems across diverse domains. Recently, the dynamic orchestration of agent systems has become an important research direction. However, existing methods suffer from limited composition, misaligned dependencies, and inflexible scale, restricting their ability to adapt to reasoning requirements during execution. To address these limitations, we reframe MAS design as a partially observable Markov decision process, in which both the composition and scale of the MAS are dynamically determined. We propose DHCG, a novel framework that coordinates three modules (Planner, Worker, and Generator) to progressively construct a dynamic hierarchical collaboration graph from scratch based on the query and evolving execution feedback. At each step, guided by feedback, the Planner generates a set of distinct and complementary roles tailored to the current reasoning needs and selectively routes relevant information to each role. It can also finalize the hierarchical collaboration graph early or progressively expand it when additional reasoning is required. We further introduce action-aware preference optimization to train the Planner to make more effective decisions when constructing hierarchical collaboration graphs. We systematically evaluate DHCG across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and domain-specific reasoning benchmarks. DHCG achieves state-of-the-art average performance among the compared methods, improving over the single-agent baseline by 13.06 points and outperforming both static and dynamic MAS baselines by 2.77-8.02 points. Additional experiments further demonstrate its generalization across different Planner backbones and unseen Worker models.

</details>


### [79] [RA-MoWE: Workflow-Affinity Embeddings for Query Clustering and Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07851)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RA-MoWE是一个框架，通过工作流亲和度嵌入对查询进行聚类，指导生成可重用的专家工作流，平衡了单一工作流优化与每个查询单独优化的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问题：单一工作流优化会忽略不同查询所需的推理策略差异；逐个查询搜索新工作流则成本高昂。目标是在两者之间找到平衡点。

Method: 1. 固定一组参考工作流，计算查询与工作流的亲和度嵌入；2. 基于嵌入聚类查询；3. 用每个聚类的查询和平均嵌入初始化/细化专家工作流；4. 嵌入编码器从查询文本预测亲和度嵌入，使新查询无需执行参考工作流即可选择专家。

Result: 在300个查询（数学、科学、编程）上测试，平均任务得分比仅选择参考工作流提高4.04个百分点，推理时语言模型调用次数减少27.7%。

Conclusion: RA-MoWE有效解决了工作流优化与查询个性化之间的权衡问题，在提升性能的同时提高了效率，展示了基于嵌入的聚类方法在引导专家工作流生成方面的优势。

Abstract: Agentic workflows enable large language models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks by coordinating reasoning, tool use, and verification. However, a workflow optimized for an entire task collection can overlook differences in the reasoning strategies that individual queries need, while searching for a new workflow for every query repeats costly optimization. To address this tradeoff, we introduce RA-MoWE, a framework that uses workflow-affinity embeddings to cluster queries and guide the generation of reusable expert workflows. Each embedding records how well a fixed set of reference workflows solves a query, revealing similarities in which reasoning strategies are effective. RA-MoWE uses each cluster's queries and average embedding to initialize and refine a specialized workflow through execution feedback. An embedding encoder predicts these embeddings from query text, allowing new queries to select a generated expert without first executing the reference workflows. On a 300-query test set drawn from four benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, and programming, RA-MoWE improves average task score by 4.04 percentage points over selecting among the reference workflows, while using 27.7% fewer language-model calls at inference.

</details>


### [80] [ShanLiangRen: A Nutrition Agent for Personalized Daily Meal Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07886)
*Miao Xie,Xiao Zhang,Yuan Wang,Ruixin Zhu,Chunli Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出个性化完全量化多目标膳食规划问题（MDP），开发ShanLiangRen营养代理系统，通过检索增强生成和帕累托原则优化生成包含食材和份量的完全量化膳食计划，实现约束满足和营养目标达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膳食营养规划在慢性病管理和健康维持中至关重要，但应用中需同时满足个性化约束和多维营养目标，两者常冲突且用户约束会随反馈演变，导致通用指南与可执行计划间存在巨大差距。

Method: 1. 将膳食规范、营养数据、用户属性和自然语言需求转换为个性化约束规划实例；2. 采用精确检索增强生成方法缩小可行候选集；3. 通过帕累托原则指导的细化过程，LLM在确定性营养计算和约束验证反馈下迭代优化候选计划。

Result: 系统输出包含明确食材和份量的完全量化膳食计划，提供营养合规性报告显示约束满足和营养区间达成；已发布为微信小程序ShanLiangRen，提供演示视频。

Conclusion: ShanLiangRen系统能有效解决个性化多目标膳食规划问题，通过检索增强生成与帕累托优化的结合，生成符合用户需求和营养目标的膳食计划。

Abstract: Dietary nutrition planning plays an important role in chronic disease management and maintaining a healthy body. In applications, it must simultaneously satisfy personalized constraints and reasonable multidimensional nutritional goals. These two aspects often conflict, and user constraints evolve with feedback, resulting in a substantial gap between generic guidelines and executable plans. To bridge this gap, we first propose the personalized fully quantified multiobjective dietary planning problem (MDP). To tackle MDP, we develop a nutrition agent, ShanLiangRen. The system first transforms dietary specifications, nutrient data, user attributes and natural language requirements into an individualized constrained planning instance. It then employs an exact retrieval-augmented generation method to shrink the feasible candidate set from a large scale ingredient and recipe space. Finally, it adopts a refinement guided by Pareto principles, where an LLM iteratively revises candidate plans under deterministic nutrition computation and feedback from constraint verification. The system outputs fully quantified meal plans with explicit ingredients and portion sizes, together with reports on nutrition compliance that show constraint satisfaction and nutrient interval attainment. We have released the system online as a WeChat Program, ShanLiangRen. A demo video is available at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=652OtY5VlGA.

</details>


### [81] [SIGMA: Self-Improving Alignment Generalization from a Model Spec](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07935)
*Jingyu Zhang,Shruti Palaskar,Daniel Khashabi,Benjamin Van Durme,Leon A. Gatys,Joseph Yitan Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM代理具备执行复杂任务和自我改进的能力，但安全对齐更难验证，存在能力增长但缺乏安全对齐的风险。SIGMA是一个数据生成和训练管道，使模型能够利用自身推理能力加强安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法专注于使用可验证反馈的能力自我改进，或使用来自更强模型或精心策划数据的监督进行对齐训练，这为对齐创造了外部监督瓶颈。研究问题：当前模型能否改进自己的安全对齐？

Method: SIGMA是一个数据生成和训练管道，使模型能够进行对齐自我改进。给定仅"模型规范"说明模型期望的行为，SIGMA首先执行规范指导的任务合成，使用候选模型作为任务设计代理生成多样化的对齐困境场景；然后进行自我判断对齐训练，通过监督微调和基于标准的强化学习，使用模型本身作为奖励模型。

Result: 尽管仅在单轮聊天数据上训练，SIGMA在多轮代理环境中显著提高了安全对齐：AgentHarm有害性从22.6降至14.8，代理不匹配从79.1降至3.8。SIGMA在Deliberative Alignment和Constitutional AI基线上表现更好，并保持通用能力。

Conclusion: 分析表明，平衡无害性和有用性的模型规范、用于安全推理的测试时推理、以及来自SIGMA任务设计代理的高质量标准是有效自我改进的关键要素。SIGMA证明了模型能够在无外部监督的情况下改进自己的安全对齐。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly capable of executing complex tasks and of recursively improving themselves on easy-to-verify objectives such as software engineering and mathematics. Since alignment is much harder to verify, this creates a growing risk of capabilities increasing without appropriate safety alignment, especially as capabilities expand to auto-research and cybersecurity. Existing approaches focus on capability self-improvement using verifiable feedback or on alignment training with supervision from stronger models or curated data, creating an external supervision bottleneck for alignment. We ask whether current models can improve their own safety alignment, and propose SIGMA, a data generation and training pipeline enabling alignment self-improvement that generalizes to out-of-distribution settings. Given only a "Model Spec" stating the model's desired behavior, SIGMA leverages a model's reasoning capabilities to strengthen its own safety reasoning. SIGMA first performs spec-guided task synthesis, using the candidate model as a task designer agent to generate diverse alignment dilemma scenarios and convert them into training tasks that stress-test its understanding of the Model Spec. Next, SIGMA conducts self-judged alignment training through supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning with the model itself as the reward model. Despite training only on single-turn chat data, SIGMA improves safety alignment in multi-turn agentic environments (AgentHarm harmfulness decreases from 22.6 to 14.8; Agentic Misalignment decreases from 79.1 to 3.8), outperforms Deliberative Alignment and Constitutional AI baselines, and retains general capability. Analyses show that a Model Spec balancing harmlessness and helpfulness, test-time reasoning for safety deliberation, and high-quality rubrics from SIGMA's task designer agent are crucial for effective self-improvement.

</details>


### [82] [Confidence Reasoning Graphs: Structured Confidence Estimation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07948)
*Brendan King,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Jean-Flavien Bussotti,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRG是一种无需特权模型访问或训练数据的推理时框架，通过将任务完成分解为基于轨迹证据的上下文化子断言并估计置信度，实现更准确的置信度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需要负责任决策的领域中，使用LLM代理时需要准确评估代理的成功概率，但证据分散在代理轨迹的异质、相互依赖步骤中，且实际部署面临LLM内部信号访问受限、代理运行成本高、训练数据不可用或快速过时等挑战。

Method: CRG从任务完成的前提出发，将整体判断分解为基于轨迹证据的上下文化子断言，对每个终端断言进行置信度估计，最后聚合得到整体置信度估计。

Result: 在三个代理基准、三种骨干模型和三种代理框架上，CRG的置信度校准度和风险感知决策能力均优于口语化、基于采样和白盒代理基线；消融实验表明改进源于断言级置信度估计和聚合而非图构建本身。

Conclusion: CRG能够暴露每个置信度估计背后的断言和轨迹证据，支持决策时的审计，且校准误差单独使用具有误导性，白盒代理基线虽校准度看似良好但实际区分能力接近随机。

Abstract: When using an LLM agent in a consequential domain, making an informed decision about whether to trust its output or intervene requires calibrated confidence in the agent's success. Confidence estimation for agents is difficult because evidence about success is distributed across heterogeneous, interdependent steps of an agent's trajectory. Practical agentic deployments introduce further challenges: frontier LLMs often provide limited access to internal signals, agent roll-outs are costly, and training data may be unavailable or quickly become outdated. To address these challenges, we introduce Confidence Reasoning Graphs (CRGs), an inference-time framework that estimates the probability an agent accomplished its task from a single trajectory, without privileged model access or training data. Rather than compressing an execution into a single holistic judgment, a CRG begins with the claim that the agent accomplished its task, decomposes it into contextualized sub-claims grounded in trajectory evidence, estimates confidence for each terminal claim, and finally aggregates these into an overall confidence estimate. Across three agentic benchmarks, three backbone models, and three agent frameworks, CRGs yield better-calibrated confidence and stronger risk-aware decision making than verbalized, sampling-based, and white-box surrogate baselines. We further find that calibration error alone can be misleading: a white-box surrogate baseline appears well calibrated while providing near-chance discrimination. Ablations attribute CRG's improvements to claim-level confidence estimation and aggregation rather than graph construction alone. Finally, a CRG exposes the claims and trajectory evidence underlying each confidence estimate, enabling it to be audited at decision time.

</details>


### [83] [Same Feedback, Different Answer: Measuring Run-to-Run Instability in Frontier-Model Customer Feedback Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08036)
*Viraj Bagal,Raviraja Ganta,Prabhath Chellingi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出重复运行评估框架，通过引入基于分类法的代理（TGA）来提高AI代理在处理非结构化数据时的输出稳定性和可重复性。TGA在客户反馈等任务中显著降低了主题 churn，保持了数量一致性，并比原始生成和分层分解方法更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理越来越多地被编程用于自动化知识工作，需要确保在底层证据不变时，代理的分类、优先级和计数在不同运行间保持一致。现有评估方法缺乏对输出稳定性的专门测量，特别是在处理非结构化数据时。

Method: 引入 repeat-run evaluation 框架，评估两个指标：theme churn（返回类别集的标准化变化）和 volume disagreement（持续类别的计数变化）。评估了三种执行设计：原始生成、无分类法分层分解、基于分类法的代理（TGA，使用持久主题、子主题和记录级预测）。

Result: 固定Claude Opus 4.8和1,000条记录语料库时，TGA相比原始生成和分层分解，主题 churn 减少86-88%，匹配主题数量零不一致。TGA比所有原始模型更稳定，稳定性优势随语料库大小增加而保持，在严格/宽松主题匹配条件下均优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 分类法 grounding 产生了更一致和可重复的输出。该框架适用于广泛的非结构化语料库重复综合任务，包括财务报告、法律文件、事件记录和科学文献，为AI代理在知识工作自动化中的可靠性评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being programmed to automate knowledge work over large collections of unstructured data. Such automation requires repeatability: when the underlying evidence is unchanged, the agent's categories, priorities, and counts should not shift materially between runs, even if each individual answer appears plausible. We introduce a repeat-run evaluation framework that aligns semantically equivalent categories and focuses on two operating metrics: theme churn, the normalized change in the returned category set, and volume disagreement, the change in counts for categories that persist. We evaluate three recurring customer-feedback tasks across eight frontier models, corpus sizes from 100 to 5,000 records, multiple prompts, and three execution designs: raw generation, taxonomy-free hierarchical decomposition, and a taxonomy-grounded agent (TGA) using persistent themes, subthemes, and record-level predictions. With Claude Opus 4.8 and the 1,000-record corpus fixed, TGA reduces theme churn by 86--88% relative to both raw generation and hierarchical decomposition, while matched-theme volumes have zero disagreement. The taxonomy-grounded agent is more stable than every raw model in the screen, remains more stable at each corpus size, and keeps this advantage when theme matching is made stricter or looser. Although evaluated on customer feedback, the framework targets repeated synthesis of unstructured corpora more broadly, including financial reports, legal documents, incident records, and scientific literature. Overall, these results show that taxonomy grounding produces more consistent and repeatable outputs for recurring knowledge work.

</details>


### [84] [DAEDALUS: Bootstrapping Agent Memory from Self-Generated Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08048)
*Antoine Edy,Max Conti,Victor Xing,Marc-Antoine Allard,Nawfal Benhamdane,Gautier Viaud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAEDALUS是一种无需现有任务或oracle验证器，通过两个代理（探索者与求解者）自我引导的探索过程构建可重用代理记忆的方法，能够显著提升LLM代理在新环境中的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在新环境中缺乏操作知识，必须自己发现工具行为或环境约定；没有过去尝试的记忆，它们会在任务间重复错误，导致更多任务失败和更长的轨迹；现有方法依赖人工编写的指南或来自训练任务和oracle验证器的程序记忆，都需要对环境的先验知识。

Method: DAEDALUS配对两个代理：探索者与交互环境生成具有挑战性但可解的任务，求解者尝试这些任务。从求解者失败中推导启发式，只有当求解者在上下文中反复成功时才接受。这些结果为探索者提供反馈以细化未来任务的难度。接受的启发式被整合到测试时使用的记忆库中。

Result: 在AppWorld、τ²-bench和AutomationBench上，相比无记忆基线，DAEDALUS提高平均成功率高达15.9个百分点，pass^5提高高达2.2倍。性能在小探索预算下就出现，启发式也帮助其他模型家族的代理。消融实验表明求解者轨迹提供推导有效启发式所需的关键信息，而早期发现的分解使探索更具成本效益。

Conclusion: DAEDALUS通过自我引导的探索和求解者反馈构建记忆库，无需外部训练数据或oracle验证器，在多个基准测试中显著提升代理性能，且推理成本低于大多数依赖训练任务的方法。生成的任务可作为基准任务的代理，用于模型性能排名。

Abstract: LLM agents often lack the operational knowledge to act reliably in new environments, as they must discover specific tool behaviors or environment conventions on their own. Without memory of past attempts, they repeat the same mistakes across tasks, leading to more task failures and longer trajectories. To address this, agentic systems typically rely on human-written guidelines or on procedural memory built from training tasks and an oracle verifier, both of which require prior knowledge of the environment. We present DAEDALUS, a method for bootstrapping reusable agent memory from self-generated practice without existing tasks or oracle verifiers. DAEDALUS pairs two agents: an explorer that interacts with the environment to generate challenging yet solvable tasks, and a solver that attempts them. A heuristic is derived from each solver failure and accepted only after the solver repeatedly succeeds with that heuristic in context. These outcomes also provide feedback for the explorer to refine the difficulty of future tasks. Accepted heuristics are then consolidated into a memory bank for test-time use. Across AppWorld, $τ^2$-bench, and AutomationBench, DAEDALUS improves mean success rates by up to 15.9 points and pass^5 by up to 2.2x over a no-memory baseline, and is competitive with methods using training tasks, at a lower inference cost than most. We show that performance gains already emerge with a small exploration budget, and that its heuristics also benefit agents from other model families. Our ablations further reveal that solver traces provide the key information needed to derive effective heuristics, while factorizing early discoveries makes exploration more cost-efficient. Beyond memory construction, we find that the tasks generated by DAEDALUS can serve as a proxy for benchmark tasks when ranking models by performance. Code and artifacts: www.github.com/illuin-tech/daedalus.

</details>


### [85] [SpeedrunBench: Challenging LLM Agents with Video Game Speedrunning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08076)
*Yoshinari Fujinuma,Keisuke Kamahori,Ryuto Koike,Abdelrahman Madkour,Varun Prashant Gangal,Monty Bichouna,Martyna Markiewicz,Shivani Jain,Duncan Curtis,Rebecca Qian,Anand Kannappan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPEEDRUNBENCH基准测试评估前沿LLM agents在视频游戏速通中的策略形成能力，通过9个不同游戏测试agents的持续改进、自我反思、知识利用和长时序推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人类开发解决方案变得不足，需要研究LLM agents在缺乏上下文和训练数据的问题上发展复杂策略的能力，探索agents能否超越人类已解决的问题。

Method: 通过视频游戏速通实践研究agents策略形成能力，提出SPEEDRUNBENCH基准测试，包含9个不同游戏，要求agents反复改进策略、反思表现、利用知识并在长时序推理中优化自身表现。

Result: 前沿agents在简单平台游戏上接近人类世界纪录，但在更长、更复杂的游戏上仍落后于人类表现；SPEEDRUNBENCH是一个饱和-resistant的评估指标，因为几乎总存在更快的完成时间等待被发现。

Conclusion: SPEEDRUNBENCH是有用的测试床，用于研究agents的策略形成能力，同时作为饱和-resistant的评估手段，证明该基准测试能有效衡量agents在复杂、开放-ended任务中的持续优化能力。

Abstract: Frontier LLM agents have been shown to be capable of solving increasingly complex tasks for which humans have measurable solutions. This begs the pertinent question of whether LLM agents can go beyond what humans have already solved. The ability to develop sophisticated strategies to tackle consequential problems becomes paramount as well-trodden, human-developed solutions become insufficient for problems for which we lack context or enough training data. We study agents' capability of such strategy formation through the communal practice of video game speedrunning. In speedrunning, practitioners compete to find the fastest way to complete a video game under certain conditions, and in so doing uncovering interesting unorthodox play styles that require a thorough understanding and mastery of the underlying game mechanics. We introduce SPEEDRUNBENCH, a benchmark that evaluates frontier LLM agents across 9 different games. To perform well in this benchmark, agents must repeatedly improve their strategy, reflect on their performance, exploit their gained knowledge, and reason across a long-horizon of actions to improve on an increasingly difficult problem: being faster than themselves and everyone else. Our experiments show that while frontier agents approach human world records in simple platformer games, they remain behind human performance on longer, more complex games under practical budgets. These results suggest that SPEEDRUNBENCH is a useful testbed for studying agents' strategy formation capabilities as well as being a saturation-resistant evaluation measure, as there is almost always a faster completion time waiting to be discovered.

</details>


### [86] [POLAR: Ontology-Guided Risk Prevention for Tool-Calling LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08082)
*Yunju Kang,Seonghyeon Cho,Irene Li,Yeo-Chan Yoon,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POLAR是一个面向小规模工具调用代理的安全防护框架，通过结构化的双层本体论评估操作的可逆性，在执行前对不符合阈值的操作进行剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM工具使用代理在动态环境中运行，许多操作存在操作风险。现有安全机制仅在错误发生后做出反应。现有预防性方法要么在思维链推理上微调代理，要么将自然语言防护栏编译成运行时检查，但缺乏结构化、可审计的裁决机制。

Method: POLAR通过结构化的双层本体论评估可逆性。它为每个操作分配一个分级可逆性分数，通过推导候选逆序序列；执行前剪枝未通过阈值的调用。

Result: 在τ²基准测试上评估了六个代理模型，POLAR在航空领域四个模型上提升了0.11-0.18点的平均任务奖励，但仅18个模型-领域组合中的8个有整体改善；零售领域和更强的代理往往出现退化。POLAR提供了可审计的结构检查并描述了任务-效用权衡。奖励不是防止伤害的直接衡量指标。

Conclusion: POLAR为工具调用代理的安全提供了新的结构化可逆性评估方法，但其有效性因领域和代理强度而异，存在安全性与效用之间的权衡。

Abstract: LLM tool-use agents operate in dynamic environments where many actions carry operational risk. However, most safety mechanisms react only after errors manifest. Existing pre-emptive approaches either fine-tune the agent on chain-of-thought deliberation or compile natural-language guardrails into runtime checks, but they do so without exposing a structural, auditable verdict. We propose POLAR, a guardrail framework for small tool-calling agents that assesses reversibility through a structured two-layer ontology. POLAR assigns each action a graded reversibility score by deriving a candidate inverse sequence; calls failing a threshold are pruned before execution. Evaluated on $τ^2$-bench across six agent models, POLAR improves mean task reward by 0.11 to 0.18 points on airline for four of six agents, but only eight of eighteen model--domain cells improve overall; retail and stronger agents often regress. POLAR provides an auditable structural check and characterizes its task-utility trade-offs. Reward is not a direct measure of prevented harm.

</details>


### [87] [DSV-Mem: Evaluating Multimodal Memory in Professional Workflows for MLLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08102)
*Jike Zhong,Ritwick Chaudhry,Xuanbai Chen,Tianchen Zhao,Linghan Xu,Yifan Xing,Nishant Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSV-Mem是一个用于评估对话式多模态大语言模型在专业工作流中处理密集状态化视觉记忆能力的基准测试。该基准包含5类1,000个专家审核的场景和问题，评估模型在追踪复杂状态变化、验证用户前提和解决冲突方面的能力。实验发现，状态演化（特别是更新数量）是影响模型性能的主要因素，而原始对话长度、OCR和算术能力并非瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式MLLM基准测试主要关注非正式的日常交互和个人生活场景，而专业场景通常涉及结构化、信息密集的工件，需要频繁修订和权威更新，以及需要精确追踪状态变化的组合式查询。因此需要一个新的基准来评估模型在专业工作流中的密集状态化视觉记忆能力。

Method: DSV-Mem基准包含专家审核的场景和1,000个问题，分为5类：当前状态、过去状态、派生状态、变更历史和冲突/拒绝。采用Hartley启发式标准选择需要更广泛视觉证据检查的问题。同时引入生成框架，将状态转换合成与对话填充解耦。在27种配置上评估前沿模型和开源模型。

Result: 最强基线模型在DSV-Mem上的得分低于45%。研究发现状态演化（特别是支配更新的数量）是影响难度的主导因素，多模态和信息密度也有贡献，但原始对话长度、OCR和算术能力不是主要瓶颈。模型在回答前常未能验证用户前提与先前状态更新的对比。增加推理努力和记忆管理方法收益有限，而状态感知设计更有效。

Conclusion: DSV-Mem基准测试和代码将公开发布，揭示了当前模型在专业场景中处理密集状态化视觉记忆的局限性。未来的模型开发应更关注状态感知设计，以更好地支持专业工作流中的复杂查询和状态追踪需求。

Abstract: Conversational MLLM agents are increasingly expected to assist in professional workflows, from AI research and engineering design to product management and business operations. Yet this capability remains underexplored: existing benchmarks largely focus on informal, everyday interactions and personal-life scenarios featuring photographic natural images, isolated static artifacts, and recall-oriented questions. In contrast, professional scenarios often involve structured, information-heavy artifacts that undergo frequent revisions and authority updates, and compositional queries requiring reconciliation of many artifact versions while tracking state precisely. To address these challenges, we introduce DSV-Mem, a benchmark for evaluating Dense Stateful Visual Memory. DSV-Mem comprises expert-reviewed scenarios and 1,000 questions across five user-oriented categories (Current State, Past State, Derived State, Change History, and Conflict/Refusal). A Hartley-inspired criterion favors questions with broader visual-evidence inspection demands. We also introduce a generation harness that produces evaluation suites by decoupling state-transition synthesis from conversation filling. Evaluation over 27 configurations spanning frontier and open-weight models and memory management methods reveals that the strongest baseline scores below 45% on DSV-Mem. Analysis surfaces findings: 1) multimodality and information density both contribute to difficulty, but state evolution, particularly the number of governing updates, is the dominant tested factor. Raw conversation/haystack length, OCR, and arithmetic are not the primary bottlenecks; 2) models often fail to verify user premises against prior state updates before answering; 3) increased reasoning effort and memory management methods yield limited gains, whereas state-aware designs prove more effective. The benchmark and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [88] [ChartBmkAgent: Harness-Governed Multi-Agent Construction of Chart QA Benchmarks from Sparse Error-Taxonomy Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08106)
*Langxi Huang,Pingping Zhang,Lanyun Zhu,Chunyang Jiang,Jiawei Shao,Haocheng Yuan,Peilin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChartBmkAgent是一种自动生成图表问答（Chart QA）诊断样本的工具，通过稀疏错误分类规范构建完整的测试用例，以评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在特定能力上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs发展迅速但基准开发滞后，无法及时调查新发现的能力差距。需要一种表达性强的任务格式（如信息丰富的图表）和按需构建过程来系统评估MLLMs的能力，特别是感知与推理的耦合能力。

Method: ChartBmkAgent将识别出的能力差距转化为目标诊断证据：从稀疏错误分类规范构建完整的Chart QA样本；中央框架控制专用agent；要求特定阶段证据与原始错误类别保持一致；记录每个接受决策的基础；确保生成样本符合外部指定的诊断目标。

Result: 在300个样本上，MLLM准确率从32.7%到84.3%，显示不同类别的能力差异。定向后续得分50.0%与匹配对照组82.2%（p=8.96×10^-6）；所有六个跨模型比较方向一致。86.4%的样本通过多个评估模型检验，证明符合指定的错误类别测试标准。

Conclusion: 生成的样本成功揭示了MLLMs的能力差异，提供了目标验证和诊断数据生成的实证证据，证明了该系统在缩短基准开发周期、将能力差距转化为针对性测试样本方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) advance rapidly, while conventional benchmark development lags behind, delaying investigation of newly observed capability gaps. Such investigation requires an expressive task format and an on-demand construction process: information-rich charts make chart question answering (Chart QA) suitable for probing coupled perception and reasoning. Automated Chart QA construction is intended to shorten the benchmark-development cycle by turning identified gaps into targeted samples on demand. Current methods, however, commonly separate target guidance from scratch generation: target-guided systems often require prepared data, charts, or templates, while scratch-generation systems primarily ensure artifact validity, without explicitly controlling whether newly synthesized requirements and content remain aligned with an externally specified diagnostic target. We introduce ChartBmkAgent, which turns an identified capability gap into targeted diagnostic evidence by constructing complete Chart QA samples from sparse error-taxonomy specifications. Throughout construction, a central harness governs specialized agents, requires stage-specific evidence of alignment with the original error category, and records the basis for each acceptance decision. On 300 taxonomy-wide samples, MLLM accuracies ranged from 32.7% to 84.3% with distinct category profiles, showing that generated samples reveal capability differences. Across three source-model comparisons, targeted follow-ups scored 50.0% versus 82.2% on matched controls ($p=8.96\times10^{-6}$); all six cross-model comparisons had the same direction, demonstrating targeted validation and diagnostic-data generation. Multiple evaluator models assessed whether each sample tested its specified error category; 86.4% met this criterion, providing empirical evidence of target preservation.

</details>


### [89] [Learn2Play Bench: How Well Do LLM Agents Learn from Experience in Unfamiliar Environments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08215)
*Yibo Li,Jinhang Qiu,Zhi Zheng,Qianyun Guo,Jiaying Wu,Shuo Ji,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Learning from experience is essential for LLM agents to adapt to unfamiliar and dynmaic environments. Evaluating this ability is therefore important for understanding how effectively agents acquire and use new knowledge. Existing benchmarks have sought to evaluate this ability, but they primarily evaluate tasks whose rules are provided in the instructions or already familiar to pretrained models, making it difficult to distinguish learning from interactions from reasoning with existing knowledge. To address this, we introduce Learn2Play Bench, a benchmark of newly designed text-based games, whose rules are novel or counterintuitive, requiring agents to acquire knowledge through interaction rather than rely solely on pretrained knowledge. These games provide reproducible feedback and automatic scoring, enabling controlled evaluation of learning across repeated attempts. We also vary game instances to test whether agents can apply what they have learned to new situations. Therefore, we evaluate how backbone models, self-evolving methods, and agent harnesses affect agents' learning ability, revealing three findings: (1) Experience retention: Retaining complete records of actions and feedback can support more effective learning than summarizing these experiences into rules or strategies. (2) Human agent gap: Top-performing human players achieve higher peak scores than the evaluated agents. Human explore more varied strategies, and repeat actions less. (3) Harness matters: With the backbone fixed, changing the harness can improve performance while reducing estimated inference cost. Together, these findings provide insights into how LLM agents learn from experience and suggest directions for future work to improve their learning ability. Project website: https://liushiliushi.github.io/learn2play-bench-website/

</details>


### [90] [OSFP4: Joint Optimization of Diagonal Smoothing and Block Scales for NVFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08231)
*Neriah Ben David,Ori Meir,Or Ordentlich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSFP4是一种针对NVFP4数据类型的创新量化方案，通过优化平滑矩阵和对角缩放项，在保持高精度的同时实现了接近原生NVFP4吞吐量（94-97%）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVFP4是LLM推理中很有吸引力的数据类型，具有紧凑存储和原生Tensor-Core加速特性，但需要仔细量化才能保持精度。现有量化方法存在精度损失问题，需要开发新的量化方案。

Method: OSFP4为每个线性投影使用对角平滑矩阵，其条目经过联合优化以最小化NVFP4下的平方矩阵乘法量化误差。该方法考虑了舍入过程（最近舍入或GPTQ风格连续干扰消除），通过分析乘性抖动FP4量化器而非固定确定性量化器来实现。

Result: 实验表明OSFP4在相应量化设置中实现了评估竞争者中最高的平均精度，同时保持约94-97%的供应商NVFP4预填充吞吐量。

Conclusion: OSFP4成功解决了NVFP4量化的精度与性能平衡问题，为LLM推理提供了高效且准确的量化方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: NVFP4 is an attractive datatype for large language model (LLM) inference, offering compact storage and native tensor-core acceleration. However, preserving accuracy using NVFP4 requires careful quantization. In this work we develop a novel quantization scheme called Optimized Smoothing and Scaling for NVFP4 (OSFP4). For each linear projection it uses a diagonal smoothing matrix whose entries are optimized to minimize the squared matrix-product quantization error under NVFP4, taking into account the rounding procedure that is used (either round-to-nearest, or GPTQ-style successive interference cancellation). This requires performing joint optimization on the smoothing entries as well as the block scales, which is facilitated by analyzing a multiplicative-dither FP4 quantizer instead of the fixed deterministic one. Experiments show that OSFP4 achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated competitors in the corresponding quantization settings, while retaining approximately 94-97\% of vendor NVFP4 prefill throughput on the measured workloads. Our code is available in https://github.com/neriahbd/OSFP4

</details>


### [91] [LeanPlan: Optimal Planning with LLM-Generated Heuristics and Admissibility Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08246)
*André G. Pereira,Augusto B. Corrêa,Felipe Meneguzzi,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanPlan 是首个在 Lean 4 中实现的可证明最优规划系统，利用 LLM 生成具有机器验证的可采纳性启发式函数，在多项基准测试中优于现有最先进的 Scorpion 规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型 (LLM) 能生成用于满意规划的启发式函数，但这些启发式函数不具备可采纳性保证，可能导致次优计划。现有方法无法确保最优性。

Method: 1) 提出首个结合 LLM 生成启发式与机器验证可采纳性的规划系统 LeanPlan；2) 采用 agentic loop 机制，利用规划器反馈迭代改进可复用的领域特定启发式、其可采纳性证明及所需领域假设；3) 在 Lean 4 中实现启发式、证明及高效规划器，并进行机器验证的 grounding 与搜索。

Result: 在 IPC 的 10 个领域及 3 个新领域上测试，任务规模可达训练任务 57 倍；GPT-5.6 Sol 在 agentic loop 中成功为所有领域生成启发式与可采纳性证明；所得启发式使 LeanPlan 通常比 Scorpion 扩展的状态更少，且整体解决的任务更多。

Conclusion: LeanPlan 成功将 LLM 生成的启发式与机器验证的可采纳性证明结合，实现了可证明最优规划，在状态扩展次数和任务求解数量上均优于现有最先进规划器，为 LLM 驱动的规划系统提供了可验证、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) can generate heuristic functions that guide search to achieve state-of-the-art performance in satisficing planning, where any plan is acceptable. However, these heuristics are not guaranteed to be admissible and can lead to suboptimal plans. We introduce LeanPlan, the first planning system that finds optimal plans with LLM-generated heuristics whose admissibility is machine-checked. Given a domain description and training tasks, an agentic loop uses planner feedback to iteratively improve a reusable domain-specific heuristic, its admissibility proof and the required domain assumptions. LeanPlan implements the heuristic, its proof and an efficient planner with machine-checked grounding and search in Lean 4. We evaluate LeanPlan on ten domains from the International Planning Competition and three new domains, using test tasks with up to 57 times as many objects as the training tasks. With GPT-5.6 Sol in the agentic loop, we successfully generate heuristics and admissibility proofs for all these domains. With the resulting heuristics, LeanPlan usually expands fewer states than the state-of-the-art Scorpion planner and solves more tasks overall.

</details>


### [92] [SquidAgent: Parallelize Wisely, Coordinate Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08647)
*Yexiong Lin,Shanshan Ye,Yu Yao,Zhen Fang,Bo Han,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: LLM-based agents solve complex multi-step tasks, but sequential execution incurs substantial latency. In principle, parallelizing work across multiple agents should yield near-linear speedups. Yet existing parallel multi-agent systems often run slower than a single-agent baseline. We attribute this gap to two hidden costs that parallel execution incurs but a serial agent avoids. First, there is a re-exploration cost: redundant effort spent by parallel workers reconstructing context that the orchestrator already possesses, such as prior decisions, that would otherwise be inherited implicitly in a serial execution. Second, there is an alignment cost: the overhead required to reconcile inconsistencies across independently generated outputs. We thus derive a principled decision criterion: a layer should be parallelized only when its critical-path cost, plus re-exploration and alignment overheads, is lower than the corresponding serial cost. While this criterion is naturally expressed in wall-clock time, we observe that LLMs are poorly calibrated when asked to estimate task duration. To address this, we instead measure cost in predicted output tokens, which we empirically find LLMs can estimate substantially more reliably than wall-clock time. Building on this token-based criterion, we propose SquidAgent. It estimates all token budgets in a single planning step, forks each worker directly from the orchestrator's session to eliminate re-exploration cost, and replaces post-hoc reconciliation with a pre-generated shared convention block that converts alignment into a bounded upfront cost. A deterministic scheduler then applies the criterion layer by layer. Empirically, SquidAgent achieves a 2.2$\times$ mean throughput improvement and a 2.6$\times$ mean wall-time speedup over Claude Code, and a 2.0$\times$ throughput improvement over the strongest multi-agent baseline.

</details>


### [93] [VeriFine: Scaling Verification for Self-Improvement in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08761)
*Zewei Zhou,Rachel Luo,Yulong Cao,Chaowei Xiao,Chensheng Peng,Boyi Li,Thomas Tian,Zheng Lian,Yan Wang,Jiaqi Ma,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Wenhao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriFine框架，通过协同进化策略、课程和法官实现持续自我改进，解决固定法官限制优化反馈和训练样本发现的问题，特别适用于具身推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进策略会不断暴露新失败模式，当前固定法官限制了优化反馈和有用训练样本的发现；在具身推理中，可靠评估需要考虑空间定位、因果推理和安全决策，挑战更为严峻。

Method: VeriFine框架包含两个循环：1) 策略改进循环 - 用标准法官诊断失败、构建自适应课程、优化策略；2) 法官改进循环 - 当进展停滞时，通过协同校准精炼法官，人类与代理解决分歧并收敛到物理推理目标标准。

Result: 在驾驶和机器人导航任务中，VeriFine实现了策略和法官能力的持续自我改进，通过强化微调和监督微调验证了连续改进能力。

Conclusion: 验证的扩展支持了策略失败模式演化过程中的持续自我改进，展示了协同进化策略、课程和法官框架的有效性。

Abstract: Self-improving policies continually expose new failure patterns, changing what their judges must be able to verify. However, current fixed judges constrain both optimization feedback and the discovery of useful training examples, limiting further self-improvement. This challenge is even more acute in embodied reasoning, where reliable evaluation must account for spatial grounding, causal reasoning, and safety-aware decision-making. We introduce VeriFine, an agent harness framework that scales verification through the co-evolution of the policy, training curriculum, and judge. The Policy Improvement Loop uses a rubric judge to diagnose recurring failures, construct an adaptive curriculum, and optimize the policy. When progress plateaus and verification becomes a bottleneck, the Judge Improvement Loop selectively queries human guidance on informative failure cases and refines the judge through coactive calibration, in which humans and agents resolve disagreements and converge toward the objective rubric of physical reasoning. The revised judge then guides the next stage of data selection and policy optimization. Experiments on driving and robot navigation tasks demonstrate continuous self-improvement in both policy and judge capability across reinforcement and supervised fine-tuning. These results show how scaling verification supports continuous self-improvement as policy failure patterns evolve.

</details>


### [94] [MedZERO: Self-Evolving Agents for Open-Ended Medical Reasoning Through Controlled Knowledge Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08327)
*Xilin Dang,Weilin Ruan,Xue Yang,Jinghao Wang,Xiaowei Hu,Jinpeng Li,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedZERO 是一个面向开放性医学推理的自进化框架，由 Examiner（生成前沿医学问答对）和 Reasoner（基于证据的多轮推理解决）组成，通过受控知识积累实现可靠、持续的性能提升，在5个基准上相较基座模型和对比方法提升最高达13.7个平均准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs 在医学问答中受限于静态参数知识和高昂专家监督成本；现有自进化方法适用于数学、编程等易验证领域，而医学推理具有开放性、知识密集且仅部分可验证的特性，需要新的框架。

Method: MedZERO 由 Examiner（生成前沿医学问题-选项对）和 Reasoner（结合外部知识工具进行证据导向的多轮推理）耦合构成，采用受控知识积累策略，维护临时探索性知识与持久性知识。

Result: 在5个公开医学推理基准上使用4B-和8B基座模型评估，MedZERO 持续优于基座模型和先前自进化基线，最高比次优自进化基线提升 13.7 个平均准确率点。

Conclusion: MedZERO 实现了开放性医学推理的可信、持续改进，证明了自进化范式在非易验证领域的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in medical question answering and clinical reasoning, yet their improvement remains constrained by static parametric knowledge and costly expert supervision. Self-evolving agents offer a promising alternative by enabling models to improve through iterative task generation and problem-solving. However, most existing self-evolving methods are designed for easily verifiable domains such as mathematics and coding, where solutions can be checked by exact answers or executable programs. Medical reasoning is fundamentally different: it is open-ended, knowledge-intensive, and often only partially verifiable. We present MedZERO, a self-evolving framework for open-ended medical reasoning. MedZERO couples an Examiner that generates frontier medical question-option pairs with a Reasoner that solves them through evidence-grounded multi-turn reasoning with external knowledge tools. To support reliable, continual improvement, MedZERO adopts controlled knowledge accumulation, which maintains temporary exploratory knowledge and curated persistent knowledge in reasoning. We evaluate MedZERO on five public medical reasoning benchmarks using 4B- and 8B-scale base models under open-ended evaluation. Across all settings, MedZERO consistently outperforms the underlying base models and prior self-evolving baselines, achieving up to 13.7 average accuracy-point gains over the next-best self-evolving baseline.

</details>


### [95] [MoF: Preference-Aware Mixture Modeling for Black-Box LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08330)
*Hun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoF是一种可扩展的个性化框架，通过共享的潜在偏好侧头建模用户偏好，实现参数高效且对未见用户也能泛化的个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒LLM个性化方法依赖用户特定评分头，导致个性化参数数量随用户数线性增长，且需要对未见用户进行额外适配。

Method: Mixture-of-Facets框架：将用户偏好建模为共享潜在偏好侧头的组合，通过基于历史记录的条件路由实现个性化，无需为未见用户更新参数。

Result: 在多种个性化任务中提供更强的个性化性能，同时相比现有方法具有更好的可扩展性和参数效率，且对未见用户表现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MoF提供了一种可扩展且参数高效的LLM个性化替代方案，有效支持对已知和未知用户的个性化。

Abstract: Proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a wide range of tasks, yet aligning their outputs with diverse user preferences remains challenging. Existing personalization approaches for black-box LLMs often rely on user-specific scoring heads, causing the number of personalized parameters to grow linearly with the number of users and requiring additional adaptation for unseen users. To address these limitations, we propose Mixture-of-Facets (MoF), a scalable personalization framework for black-box LLMs that models user preferences as compositions of shared latent preference facets rather than dedicated user-specific parameters. MoF performs personalization through history-conditioned routing over shared facet heads, enabling personalization for users unseen during training without additional parameter updates. Across diverse personalization tasks, MoF delivers stronger personalization performance while maintaining a more scalable and parameter-efficient design than prior approaches. Additional analysis indicates strong generalization to unseen users.

</details>


### [96] [Transect: Retaining Observability for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08364)
*Toby D. Pilditch,Konstantinos Voudouris,Alexandra Abbas,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Transect是一个开源工具包，基于Inspect Scout构建，用于分析AI评估中的长篇复杂代理行为记录，通过统一时间线整合事件、标记和子代理活动，帮助评估人员保持科学严谨性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前沿AI评估采用开放式、代理式、长周期任务，其行为记录可达数百页，评估者的可观测性范围不断缩小。虽然语言模型助手可以帮助分类和解释行为，但也会赋予人类评估者过大的分析自由度，威胁基于语言模型的行为分析的再现性和可审计性。评估人员需要工具来处理更长的复杂AI评估，同时保持科学严谨性。

Method: Transect基于Inspect Scout构建，提供可复用的评估配置文件（包含任务上下文和行为词汇表），将判断模型和分析设置分开提供。其导航式报告在统一的基于回合的时间线上对齐记录事件、标记使用、子代理活动和模型生成的行为标签。用户可以快速掌握运行叙事，将任何标签或事件追溯到其源回合，并导出基础数据表进行跨运行分析。

Result: 在生成近1300万token的AI研发评估中演示了该工作流程，将代理工作分为与研究技能分类、子代理委托和交互、标记使用一致的行为阶段。综合视图显示代理工作集中于操作工作和手稿生产，几乎没有持续的假设生成阶段——这可能是高质量科学输出的必要组成部分。

Conclusion: Transect的灵活、可定制转录分析管道使评估人员能够跟上更长、更复杂、更频繁的AI评估，同时支持科学严谨性、透明度和可重复性。该工具通过提供统一的可视化分析和可追溯性，解决了长篇代理记录分析中的关键挑战。

Abstract: Frontier AI evaluations increasingly use open-ended, agentic, long-horizon tasks whose transcripts can span hundreds of pages of outputs and actions from complex multi-agent networks. The observability envelop-the range of what evaluators can reliably infer about an agent's behaviours-is therefore narrowing. Language model assistants can help classify and interpret agent behaviour but also afford human evaluators significant analytical degrees of freedom, threatening the reproducibility and auditability of language-model-based transcript analysis. Transect is an open source package built on Inspect Scout to help evaluators understand how a long agent run unfolded, identify behaviour worth investigating, and check interpretations against the transcript. Users specify task context and behavioural vocabulary in a reusable evaluation-family configuration, with judge models and analysis settings supplied separately. Transect's navigable reports align recorded events, token use, sub-agent activity, and model-generated behavioural labels on a common turn-based timeline. Reviewers can quickly grasp a run's narrative, trace any label or event to its source turns, and export the underlying data tables for cross-run analysis. We demonstrate the workflow on an AI R&D evaluation that generated almost 13 million tokens, dividing the agents' work into behavioural phases aligned with research-skill classifications, sub-agent delegations and interactions, and token use. The combined view shows a focus on operational work and manuscript production, with little evidence of a sustained hypothesis generation stage-arguably a necessary component for high-quality scientific outputs. Transect's flexible, customisable transcript-analysis pipeline will enable evaluators to keep pace with longer, more complex, more frequent AI evaluations while supporting scientific rigour, transparency, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [97] [EMHO: EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization via Experience Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08432)
*Hyun Jung Lee,Jungtaek Kim,Jongwon Jeong,Tae-Eui Kam,Donghyun Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EMHO是一种自进化框架，通过分析执行轨迹和历史来迭代改进控制规划、上下文和工具使用的harness，在EmbodiedBench上提升了Qwen 9B和27B模型在导航和操作任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前改进具身智能体通常侧重于优化底层模型，而控制规划、上下文和工具使用的harness通常通过工程设计。作者提出疑问：这个harness能否直接从稀疏环境反馈的经验轨迹中自我改进，而无需重新训练底层模型。

Method: 提出EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization (EMHO)框架，保持具身模型冻结，通过分析执行轨迹和先前的harness历史迭代修改harness。引入EMHO-Merge解决多子任务共享harness的优化权衡问题，利用episode级别的收益和损失指导修订行为的应用时机和方式。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench的导航和操作任务上，EMHO持续提高了Qwen 9B和27B模型的任务成功率。定性分析显示EMHO不仅从失败中恢复，还从根本上重塑了智能体与环境的交互方式。

Conclusion: EMHO成功实现了harness的自进化，证明harness可以通过经验轨迹自我优化，无需重新训练底层模型，能够超越简单的失败恢复，从根本上改变智能体解释和交互环境的方式。

Abstract: Improving embodied agents often focuses on optimizing the underlying model through training, while the surrounding agent harness that controls planning, context, and tool use is typically engineered. We ask whether this harness can instead improve itself directly from experience traces under sparse environmental feedback. We propose EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization (EMHO), a self-evolving framework that keeps the embodied model frozen and iteratively revises its harness by analyzing execution trajectories and prior harness history. EMHO optimizes beyond skills or recovery prompts, modifying how the agent monitors progress, uses vision tools, grounds observations, and responds to failures. To support multiple subtasks with a single harness, we introduce EMHO-Merge, which addresses trade-offs in jointly optimizing a single shared harness across subtasks by using episode-level gains and losses to guide evidence-supported refinement of when and how revised behaviors are applied. We evaluate EMHO on EmbodiedBench across navigation and manipulation tasks, and EMHO consistently improves task success for both Qwen 9B and 27B models. Qualitative analysis shows that EMHO goes beyond recovering from failures and unproductive actions to reshape how the embodied agent interprets and interacts with its environment.

</details>


### [98] [AssemState: Manual and Physical-State-Guided Reasoning for Zero-shot Furniture Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08446)
*Zhiyuan Qi,Jierui Li,Yifan Shen,Cheng Qian,Jiateng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AssemState是一个零样本框架，用于从图解手册中恢复家具组装步骤并计算零件的6D位姿，通过锚点引导的边界组装状态和迭代后状态反馈细化来提升组装树恢复和零件级位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉理解方面取得了进展，但在与物理环境结合的精确3D空间推理方面仍存在困难。家具组装不仅需要从图解手册中恢复步骤级操作，还需要将语义附着关系转化为6D位姿更新，使零件能够与环境和已组装组件进行物理交互。

Method: AssemState首先采用锚点引导的边界组装状态将手册页面分解为单零件操作并恢复组装树；然后使用迭代后状态反馈细化来指导连续的SE(3)更新和修正，并通过基于模拟的释放测试验证其物理合理性。

Result: 与最强基线相比，AssemState将组装树恢复的F1从38.58%提高到62.80%，树精确匹配从28.24%提高到53.92%。在243个独立评估的零件级操作中，迭代细化使判定接受的操作从0提高到5.3%，平均Chamfer距离从5.4111降至1.7744。

Conclusion: AssemState改善了操作结构恢复和选定的局部位姿指标，但MLLM在空间关系推理方面仍然有限。视觉上合理的候选位姿可能仍然存在碰撞、漂浮、镜像方向错误、不完全座入和错误面附着等问题。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding, but precise 3D spatial reasoning integrated with physical environment remains difficult. Furniture assembly requires not only recovering step-level operations from diagrammatic manuals, but also translating semantic attachment relations into 6D pose updates that enable parts to physically interact with the environment and previously assembled components. To study this problem, we propose AssemState, a zero-shot framework for manual and physical-state-guided furniture assembly. It firstly employs anchor-guided boundary assembly states to decompose manual pages into single-part operations and recover an assembly-tree. Then, it uses iterative after-state feedback refinement to guide successive (SE(3)) updates and corrections, and validates their physical plausibility through simulation-based release tests. Experiments show that compared with the strongest prior baseline, AssemState improves F1 from 38.58\% to 62.80\% and Tree Exact Match from 28.24\% to 53.92\% for assembly-tree recovery. On 243 independently evaluated part-level operations, our proposed iterative refinement improves judge-accepted operations from 0 to 5.3\% and reduces mean Chamfer distance from 5.4111 to 1.7744. However, visually plausible candidate poses may still suffer from collision, floating, mirror-orientation errors, incomplete seating, and wrong-side attachment. These results show that AssemState improves operation-structure recovery and selected local pose metrics, while MLLMs remain limited for spatial relationship reasoning.

</details>


### [99] [Adaptive Power Sampling for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08563)
*Bingnan Xiao,Chenhao Yang,Bingcong Li,Wei Ni,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应功率采样（APS），通过根据每个查询的自我奖励差异动态调整锐化指数，在不增加训练的情况下提升大语言模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功率采样方法在所有查询上均匀锐化输出分布，忽视了查询难度差异和基础模型对每个查询的现有处理能力。

Method: 理论分析表明进一步锐化的收益取决于正确与错误回答之间的自我奖励差距。基于此洞察，APS在测试时根据答案一致性与模型自我奖励的关系，针对每个查询动态调整锐化指数。

Result: 在MATH500、HumanEval和GPQA等多个推理任务上，APS consistently outperforms使用固定锐化指数的功率采样方法，无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 自适应功率采样通过根据查询难度和模型现有能力调整锐化策略，有效提升了大语言模型的推理性能。

Abstract: Sequence-level power sampling has recently emerged as a training-free approach to reasoning by sampling from a sharpened output distribution of a base large language model (LLM). Nevertheless, existing methods typically sharpen the base model distribution uniformly across queries, overlooking variations in query difficulty and in how well the base model already handles each query. The goal of this work is to equip power sampling with query adaptivity. Theoretically, we show that the benefits of further sharpening are determined by the self-reward gap between correct and incorrect responses. Based on this insight, we propose \emph{Adaptive Power Sampling} (APS), which adjusts the sharpening exponent on a per-query basis at test time using the relationship between answer agreement and the model's self-reward. Experiments across diverse reasoning tasks, including MATH500, HumanEval, and GPQA, show that APS consistently outperforms power sampling with a fixed sharpening exponent, without additional training.

</details>


### [100] [Parallel Predictive World Models for Accurate and Efficient Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08627)
*Wanjin Feng,Baobin Zhang,Ao Yu,Shibo Feng,Xi Wang,Xingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并行预测世界模型（PPWM），通过并行预测有限时域轨迹并保持因果交互，取代自回归状态级递归预测，在长时域预测误差、CEM规划成功率及规划速度上均优于传统自回归方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域世界模型规划通常采用自回归滚动预测，虽能保持时间结构，但会产生序列路径并使后期预测暴露于递归解码状态反馈，可能影响预测准确性和规划效率。

Method: 并行预测世界模型（PPWM）并行预测有限时域轨迹，同时保持未来表示间的因果交互；每个时域基于其因果动作前缀条件化，未来表示在解码前进行交互，从而将时间因果性与逐状态输出递归分离。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务中，PPWM实现最低长时域预测误差和最高CEM模拟器成功率，相比自回归LeWM基线，CEM规划速度平均提升超过3倍。

Conclusion: 准确且高效的长时域世界模型规划无需逐状态自回归，可通过并行因果轨迹预测实现。

Abstract: Long-horizon world-model planning typically relies on autoregressive rollouts, where predicted states are repeatedly fed back into the model. This preserves temporal structure but creates a horizon-length sequential path and exposes later predictions to recursive decoded-state feedback. We introduce Parallel Predictive World Models (PPWM), which predict a finite-horizon trajectory in parallel while retaining causal interaction among future representations. Each horizon is conditioned on its causal action prefix, and future representations interact before decoding, separating temporal causality from state-by-state output recursion. We formalize this distinction by viewing autoregressive rollout as a causal trajectory map and identifying the decoded-state feedback pathway removed by PPWM. Across four visual-control tasks, PPWM achieves the lowest long-horizon prediction error and the highest Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) simulator success among the evaluated predictive interfaces. Meanwhile, PPWM achieves more than a 3$\times$ average CEM planning speedup over the autoregressive LeWM baseline. These results suggest that accurate and efficient long-horizon world-model planning does not require state-by-state autoregression, but can instead be achieved through parallel causal trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [101] [WorldSolver: Can LLM Agents Simulate the Physical Dynamics via Solver Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08720)
*Siru Jiang,Yongzhe Lyu,Shuo Lu,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Yue Liao,Bin Wang,Jian Liang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldSolver是一个包含168个物理模拟任务的基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体生成可执行求解器的能力，涵盖执行检查、视觉保真度和物理合理性三个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在科学和工程问题解决中日益重要，物理模拟是测试其能力的重要测试床。但构建求解器需要物理理解、数学推理和软件工程能力，而LLM在这方面的能力仍被低估。

Method: WorldSolver包含来自61篇经典计算机图形学论文的168个模拟任务，覆盖7个物理领域。每个任务提供固定模拟环境的代码脚手架，求解器实现由智能体完成。评估维度包括执行检查（成功执行）、视觉保真度（重现动态行为）和物理合理性（物理验证）。

Result: 生成可执行求解器已较困难，满足视觉和物理正确性更难。GPT-5.6-Sol和Claude-Opus-5表现相对较好，但总体得分仅48.7%和46.7%。大多数智能体在三个维度上都存在显著不足。

Conclusion: WorldSolver是智能体求解器生成的早期步骤，有助于推动能够忠实模拟动态物理世界的智能体发展。该基准测试揭示了当前LLM在物理模拟方面的局限性，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly advancing scientific and engineering problem solving, with physics simulation emerging as a challenging yet practical testbed for reproducing complex physical phenomena with application in embodied AI, games and films. As the workhorse of such simulation, a solver computes how the state of a dynamic system evolves over time. Building such solvers requires physical understanding to identify appropriate models, mathematical reasoning to formulate the underlying dynamics, and software engineering to implement them as executable code, yet this capability of LLM agents remains underexplored. To this end, we introduce WorldSolver, a benchmark of 168 simulation tasks derived from physical phenomena in 61 classic computer graphics papers, spanning 7 physical domains. Each task contains a code scaffold that provides a fixed simulation environment for the scene, with the solver implementation left for the agent to complete. Specifically, we evaluate them along three dimensions: Execution Checks for successful execution, Visual Fidelity for reproducing the intended dynamic behavior in the rendered simulation, and Physical Plausibility for physics-grounded verification of the generated dynamics. Experiments on frontier agents reveal that producing executable solvers is difficult itself, and satisfying visual and physical correctness is even harder. GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude-Opus-5 perform comparatively better than the other evaluated agents, yet achieve overall scores of only 48.7% and 46.7%, respectively. WorldSolver is an early step toward agentic solver generation, and we hope it helps drive progress toward agents that can faithfully simulate the dynamic physical world. Code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/WorldSolver.

</details>


### [102] [Agent in a Bottle: Can LLM Agents Turn Their Capabilities Into Cheap, Scalable Artifacts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08775)
*Ankit Sonthalia,Haritz Puerto,Alexander Rubinstein,Martin Gubri,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BOTTLELD基准测试评估LLM智能体将通用能力转化为低成本、可复用解决方案的能力，即"bottling"能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在解决特定任务时，对数百万相关实例分别查询成本过高，研究LLM智能体是否能自主创建更便宜的解决方案。

Method: BOTTLED基准测试中，智能体接收未标记的工作负载，必须在固定的时间、计算和LLM API预算下完成任务。智能体可选择自己的方法，如训练小模型或编写可复用程序。

Result: 强大的零样本任务性能并不能可靠转化为强大的bottling能力。48/60次运行得分低于其模型零样本性能的95%置信区间下界。31/60次运行低于两个小模型蒸馏基线。然而bottling可产生显著节省：Opus 5在查询产品相关性分类任务中，以657倍更低成本保留了约82%的零样本宏观F1分数，且与专门为廉价重复推理设计的"系统一"模型Jev竞争激烈。

Conclusion: BOTTLED为评估和改进智能体在有限资源下为大型重复性工作负载投资可复用解决方案的能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve many narrow tasks, but querying them separately for millions of related instances can be prohibitively expensive. Can LLM agents autonomously create cheaper solutions for such workloads? We call this ability "bottling": the ability to turn general capabilities into task-specific solutions that balance answer quality and amortised cost. We introduce BOTTLED, a benchmark in which agents receive an entire unlabelled workload and must complete it under fixed time, compute and LLM API budgets. Agents choose their own approach, such as training a small model or writing a reusable program. Across ten models and three tasks, we find that strong zero-shot task performance does not reliably translate into strong bottling capabilities. Models with similar zero-shot scores can differ substantially after bottling, and 48 of 60 bottling runs score below the lower bound of the 95% confidence interval of their model's zero-shot performance. Moreover, 31 of 60 runs underperform the stronger of two small-model distillation baselines with the same token budget. Nevertheless, bottling can yield substantial savings: on query-product relevance classification, Opus 5 retains about 82% of its zero-shot macro-F1 at roughly 657 times lower reported cost. Bottling is also competitive with Jev, a "system one" model built especially for cheap, repetitive inference: Opus 5 on the same task recovers about 94% of Jev's macro-F1 at a quarter of Jev's projected full-workload cost. BOTTLED provides a basis for evaluating and improving agents' ability to invest limited resources in reusable solutions for large, repetitive workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [When Does External Guidance Help LLM Reasoning? A Bias-Variance Theory of Guidance-Augmented GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06861)
*Sofia Torres,Gabriel Almeida,Carter Adams,Camila Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GA-GRPO 是一个统一的理论框架，将外部引导视为随机引导算子 G，分析 RLVR 中的有偏策略梯度估计器，提供收敛率、偏差界和最优权重公式，并在数学推理任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 是大语言模型多步推理的主流范式，LUFFY、ExPO、PAPO、TAPO 等方法引入外部引导信号（专家轨迹、自解释、检索思维模式），但缺乏收敛率、偏差界和最优权重规则。

Method: 将外部引导视为随机引导算子 G 重写问题分布，分析策略梯度估计器为有偏 on-policy 估计器，偏差由引导分布与策略分布之间的总变差分歧 delta_G 界定；框架包含 GRPO、LUFFY、ExPO、PAPO、TAPO 作为特例；在平滑和有界分歧假设下证明收敛率 O(1/√T)，推导 MSE 最优权重 lambda-star(T, delta, sigma₀²) = sigma₀² / (sigma₀² + R_max² delta² T)。

Result: 理论证明 GA-GRPO 收敛到 GRPO 稳定点 O(delta√T) 邻域，偏差项为 Ω(delta²T) 不可避免；在 Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Base 上跨九个数学和 OOD 基准，最优权重 GA-GRPO 匹配或超越 TAPO、LUFFY、ExPO 和 vanilla GRPO，所需 GPU 时长减少 31%。

Conclusion: 为 RLVR 引入外部引导的统一理论框架，提供收敛保证、偏差分析和最优权重设计，兼顾理论严谨性和实证性能，显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for eliciting multi-step reasoning in large language models, and a recent wave of methods (LUFFY, ExPO, PAPO, TAPO) further augments RL with \emph{external guidance} - expert traces, self-explanations, or retrieved thought patterns. Although each method reports empirical gains, none provides convergence rates, bias bounds, or an optimal weighting rule for the guidance signal. We close this gap with \emph{Guidance-Augmented GRPO} (GA-GRPO), a unified theoretical framework that casts external guidance as a stochastic guidance operator G re-writing the question distribution, and analyses the resulting policy-gradient estimator as a biased on-policy estimator whose bias is bounded by the total-variation guidance divergence delta\_G between the guidance-augmented sampling distribution and the policy's own distribution. The framework subsumes vanilla GRPO, LUFFY, ExPO, PAPO, and TAPO as special cases obtained by particular choices of G. Under smoothness and bounded-divergence assumptions we prove that GA-GRPO converges at rate O(1/sqrt(T)) to an O(delta sqrt(T))-neighbourhood of the GRPO stationary point, derive the closed-form MSE-optimal guidance weight lambda-star(T, delta, sigma\_0 squared) = sigma\_0 squared / (sigma\_0 squared + R\_max squared delta squared T), and prove a matching minimax lower bound showing the Omega(delta squared T) bias term is unavoidable. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Base across nine math and OOD benchmarks confirm that optimal-weight GA-GRPO matches or surpasses TAPO, LUFFY, ExPO, and vanilla GRPO while requiring 31\% fewer GPU-hours, and eight analysis experiments validate each theoretical prediction.

</details>


### [104] [Learning from Unreliable Trajectories: Adversarially-Robust Federated Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06918)
*Sreejeet Maity,Aritra Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在部分代理行为恶意且传输 corrupted 信息时，联邦强化学习中的样本效率收益是否能保留。提出了 Robust Async-Fed-Q 算法，通过在代理处结合 Bellman 优化算子的方差缩减估计和在服务器端使用鲁棒聚合，在保持诚实代理统计收益的同时容忍恶意攻击。论文建立了高概率有限时间界限、信息论下界，并扩展到单轨迹马尔可夫采样和异构部分覆盖场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习中的代理协作能提高样本效率，但当部分代理行为恶意并传输 corrupted 信息时，这些收益能否保留？需要理解恶意代理对协作性能的影响及可容忍的攻击强度。

Method: 提出 Robust Async-Fed-Q 算法：基于 epoch 的联邦学习算法，在代理端结合 Bellman 优化算子的方差缩减估计，在服务器端采用鲁棒聚合方法。进一步扩展到单轨迹马尔可夫采样和异构部分覆盖场景。

Result: 1) 建立了高概率有限时间界限，证明方法能保持诚实代理的统计收益并容忍恶意攻击；2) 恶意代理的影响随诚实代理数据量增加而减小，在无限样本极限下消失；3) 首次提供了近乎匹配的上界和下界；4) epoch 设计显著改善了异步采样下联邦 Q-learning 的通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文首次为对抗鲁棒联邦强化学习提供了近乎匹配的上界和下界，证明了在恶意代理存在的情况下仍能保留协作的样本效率收益。提出的 Robust Async-Fed-Q 算法在有限时间内提供理论保证，且其 epoch 设计显著提升了通信复杂度。扩展框架支持更复杂的采样场景和异构覆盖情况，为实际部署提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study federated reinforcement learning in which multiple agents interact with a common Markov decision process and communicate through a central server to collaboratively learn the optimal state-action value function. Our goal is to understand whether the sample-efficiency benefits of collaboration can be retained when a fraction of the agents behave adversarially and transmit arbitrarily corrupted information. To address this problem, we introduce Robust Async-Fed-Q, an epoch-based federated learning algorithm that combines variance-reduced estimation of the Bellman optimality operator at the agents with robust aggregation at the server.
