The model identifies missing evidence and determines the target objects, interactions, and modalities, while a physical interface executes the selected interactions and collects the multi-sensory feedback. To support this capability, we construct ROMI-2K, a large-scale real-world multi-sensory object interaction dataset covering nearly 2,000 objects and 6 atomic interactions with synchronized sensory feedback. Building on these data, we develop a two-stage training framework that aligns sensory modalities and equips the LLM to assess evidence sufficiency, select informative interactions, and reason over the multi-sensory feedback. We further characterize active perception as perception chains, where acquired evidence guides subsequent interactions and reasoning, and establish ROMA Bench to evaluate single-attribute, long-horizon multi-attribute, and intent-driven active perception. Experiments show that ROMA can actively acquire missing evidence and solve complex, long-chain multi-sensory perception tasks that existing methods struggle to handle, laying a strong perceptual foundation for active multi-sensory embodied agents.

</details>


### [139] [ProactiveVLA: Augmenting Embodied Memory through Proactive Environment Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06999)
*Shizuo Tian,Haodong Luo,Yutong Li,Yuebing Song,Yunxin Liu,Yuanchun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProactiveVLA是一个结合推理代理与冻结VLA的系统，通过主动环境探索在有限交互预算下获取可复用知识，用于部署时适应新环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速适应新环境需要机器人从有限经验中获取有用知识。现有系统通过执行反馈和记忆进行适应，但重复练习目标任务可能只完善熟悉方案，而忽略对变化条件重要的其他交互。

Method: 在完成初始任务后，代理将剩余交互预算分配给主动设定的目标，覆盖物体功能、状态改变交互及交互组合。系统验证执行结果，并将任务导向和探索性经验整合到记忆中，用于指导后续规划和控制。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro和RoboCasa365 Composite-Seen数据集上，ProactiveVLA在相同回合预算下优于基线。在LIBERO-Pro Goal-T上，最多允许一次VLA原语调用时，ProactiveVLA完成48%的实例，而最先进任务细化基线仅完成19%。

Conclusion: 主动探索策略能有效在有限交互预算下获取可复用知识，显著提升机器人对新环境的适应能力，特别是在需要精确控制VLA原语调用的场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Rapid adaptation to a new environment requires a robot to acquire useful knowledge about local objects, states, and interactions from limited experience. Systems that combine a reasoning agent with a frozen vision-language-action model (VLA) can adapt through execution feedback and memory, making the choice of experience central to their effectiveness. Repeated practice of a target task may refine a familiar solution while leaving other interactions relevant to changed conditions untested. We introduce ProactiveVLA, which uses proactive environment exploration to acquire reusable knowledge for deployment-time adaptation. After completing an initial task, the agent allocates the remaining interaction budget to self-proposed goals covering object affordances, state-changing interactions, and compositions of interactions. It verifies execution outcomes and consolidates both task-directed and exploratory experience into memory that guides subsequent planning and control. ProactiveVLA outperforms the baselines under the same turn budget on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365 Composite-Seen. On LIBERO-Pro Goal-T, with at most one VLA primitive invocation allowed during evaluation, ProactiveVLA completes 48% of instances, compared with 19% for the state-of-the-art task-refinement baseline.

</details>


### [140] [ReDex: Repairing Sim-to-Real Dexterous Policies by Finger-Level Compliant Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07525)
*Jinzhou Li,Hadi Tabatabaee,Kelin Yu,Yuyin Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo,Roberto Martín-Martín,Nima Fazeli,X. Alice Wu,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReDex是一种从仿真到真机的框架，通过人类修正接触失败并引入触觉反馈，将仿真训练的灵巧操作策略适配到真实世界，大幅提升了物体翻转和螺丝刀旋转任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的灵巧操作策略往往因接触时机和力调节错误而在真机中失效，但保留有用的多手指协调能力。需要一种方法在纠正接触失败的同时保留基础策略的协调性。

Method: ReDex框架包含三部分：1) 仿真训练的本体感知基础策略；2) 人类操作员在合规控制下对选定手指进行物理修正；3) 冻结的基础策略控制其余手指。通过结合基础策略执行、人类修正运动和指尖力观测，重建力信息目标，并使用行为克隆训练独立的力条件策略。

Result: 在两个接触密集的灵巧操作任务中，物体翻转成功率从14%提升至86%（两个物体），螺丝刀旋转进度从26.0%提升至95.3%（三个物体），显著优于直接迁移的基础策略。

Conclusion: ReDex减少了人类修正工作量，实现了从真实世界交互中学习接触调节的能力，且无需触觉仿真或复杂全手遥操作即可将力反馈引入本体感知策略。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation policies trained in simulation often fail to transfer to the real world because of errors in contact timing and force regulation. Yet these policies can retain useful multi-finger coordination for task progression. We propose ReDex, a framework for adapting a simulation-trained base policy to the real world by correcting local contact failures and incorporating tactile feedback. Starting from a proprioception-only base policy, ReDex allows a human operator to physically correct contact failures at selected fingers under compliant control during real-world rollouts, while the frozen base policy continues to control the remaining fingers. These rollouts combine base policy execution, human-corrected finger motion, and fingertip force observations. We reconstruct force-informed targets from these rollouts to train a standalone force-conditioned policy via behavior cloning. This design reduces human correction effort, enables learning of contact regulation from real-world interaction, and introduces force feedback into a proprioception-only policy without tactile simulation or complex full-hand teleoperation. We evaluate ReDex on two challenging, contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks on real hardware. Compared with sim-to-real transferred base policies, ReDex increases Object Flipping success rate from 14\% to 86\% across two objects and average Screwdriver Rotation progress from 26.0% to 95.3% across three objects.

</details>


### [141] [Nine Trials to Recover: A Reproducible Benchmark for Repertoire-Free Soft-Robot Damage Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07616)
*Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种软体机器人损伤修复的无先验基准测试，通过高斯过程期望改进（GP-EI）控制器在75个身体样本上显著优于多种基线方法，并验证了可重放控制器和冻结群体评估框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了建立一种无需预先掌握损伤先验知识的标准化方法来评估软体机器人损伤修复，将方法开发与在新机体上的评估分离，为未来学习损伤先验和自适应方法提供评估基准。

Method: 采用无先验基准协议，使用高斯过程期望改进（GP-EI）控制器结合三个初始化探测和六个反馈选定的滚动执行来调整执行器相位；包含事后确认固定控制器重放、严格无额外需求部署门限、以及冻结开发压力测试；在75个身体样本上进行评估并使用配对置信区间比较。

Result: 在两个独立的75个身体队列中，GP-EI在同等预算下优于随机搜索、Sobol、CEM和CMA-ES；在69/75个确认身体上提升最差遮罩增益，超过基线0.235-0.310平均最差遮罩奖励；平均恢复5.06个体素宽度，最差遮罩p99几何边缘应变增加0.0031；在严格部署门限下保留48.7%的平均增益；在冻结压力测试中提升71/75个身体并超过TuRBO 0.356平均最差遮罩增益。

Conclusion: 可重放控制器、身体级别推断和冻结群体评估为衡量学习损伤先验和未来自适应方法的附加值提供了参考基准。

Abstract: We present a repertoire-free benchmark for soft-robot damage recovery under nine online trials. The protocol pairs damage masks within each morphology, retains a measured nominal fallback, and separates method development from evaluation on new bodies. A Gaussian-process expected-improvement (GP-EI) reference controller adapts actuator phases using three initialization probes and six feedback-selected rollouts. Across two disjoint 75-body cohorts, it outperforms random search, Sobol, CEM, and CMA-ES under equal budgets. GP-EI improves the worst-mask gain on 69/75 confirmation bodies and exceeds these four baselines by 0.235-0.310 mean worst-mask reward. A TuRBO-style local GP is the closest comparator; the paired confidence interval includes zero. Post-confirmation fixed-controller replay finds 5.06 voxel widths of mean recovery together with a 0.0031 increase in worst-mask p99 geometric edge strain; a strict no-added-demand deployment gate retains 48.7% of mean gain. In a frozen development stress test that physically removes 10% of occupied voxels, GP-EI improves 71/75 bodies and exceeds official BoTorch TuRBO by 0.356 mean worst-mask gain. Together, the replayable controllers, body-level inference, and frozen-cohort evaluation provide a reference for measuring the added value of learned damage priors and future adaptation methods.

</details>


### [142] [TacZero: Training-Free Peg Insertion Using a General-Purpose Vision-Language Model with Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07621)
*Kazutoshi Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TacZero，利用预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）使机器人无需任务特定训练或手设计规则，即可通过解读视觉和触觉观察来执行接触丰富的操作任务。在圆柱销插入实验中，TacZero在带触觉输入时成功率达75%（15/20），无触觉输入时为50%（10/20），为通用VLM应用于接触丰富操作提供了起点并指出了相关挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接触丰富操作方法需要设计估计模型、触觉反馈控制律或从触觉数据学习模型（如物体运动估计、动作-结果预测、动作选择）。作者希望开发能从语言指令和感官观察自主确定动作的机器人，以执行接触丰富操作任务，而无需任务特定训练或手设计规则。

Method: TacZero使用预训练的通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）来解读视觉和触觉观察并选择机器人动作。系统将相机图像、机器人状态和三轴触觉响应（数值或向量叠加在图像上）输入VLM，VLM生成目标末端执行器位置和抓手开闭的命令，由底层控制器执行。

Result: 在真实世界圆柱销插入实验中，TacZero在带数值触觉输入时成功15/20次（成功率75%），无触觉输入时成功10/20次（成功率50%）。

Conclusion: 该研究为使用通用VLM进行接触丰富操作提供了具体起点，并指出了在这一方向上面临的挑战。

Abstract: Robots that autonomously determine their actions from language instructions and sensory observations could perform new contact-rich manipulation tasks without task-specific training or hand-designed rules. To perform these tasks, robots must infer how objects contact one another and move as a result, then select actions. For contact inference and action selection, prior approaches involve designing estimation models and tactile feedback control laws, or learning models for object-motion estimation, action-outcome prediction, and action selection from tactile data. Instead, we propose TacZero, which uses a pretrained general-purpose vision-language model (VLM) to interpret visual and tactile observations and select robot actions without additional tactile or manipulation training or task-specific rules for contact interpretation or action selection. TacZero provides the VLM with camera images, robot state, and three-axis tactile responses represented as numerical values or vectors overlaid on the images. From these observations and interaction history, the VLM generates commands specifying target end-effector positions and gripper opening or closing, which a low-level controller executes. In real-world cylindrical-peg insertion experiments, TacZero succeeded in 15 of 20 trials with numerical tactile input, compared with 10 of 20 without tactile input. This study provides a concrete starting point for further research on contact-rich manipulation using general-purpose VLMs and highlights challenges in pursuing this direction.

</details>


### [143] [OntoPlan: An Ontology-Grounded Scene Representation and Agentic Framework for Scalable Robot Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07649)
*Hyeongwoo Nam,Woongje Cho,Juwon Kim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OntoPlan通过基于本体论的符号化场景表示和智能体框架，在大规模室内环境中实现了高效的长时机器人任务规划，在成功率上提升显著，同时大幅降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在机器人任务规划中虽然对开放性指令跟随有潜力，但在大环境的长时任务中表现不佳。主要挑战包括：1）文本传递空间信息时难以捕获空间上下文；2）token成本随环境大小增长；3）直接用LLM生成动作序列难以满足当前世界状态和动作前提条件。

Method: 提出OntoPlan框架，包含两个核心组件：1）本体论驱动的场景表示（ontology-grounded scene representation），将对象、空间、关系和状态对齐到共享的符号词汇中；2）智能体框架，能够解释指令、选择性检索任务相关信息、形式化目标和约束、生成可执行计划。同时支持对模糊或不可行指令的灵活处理（询问后续问题或报告信息不足）。

Result: 在150个跨5个室内环境和3个场景规模的一般任务测试中，OntoPlan达到0.89平均任务成功率，对比最强基线（0.27）提升显著；每任务平均使用18.1k token，比最高效基线少约5.6倍。随着场景规模增加，OntoPlan的优势持续，而先前方法在成功率和token使用上急剧下降。

Conclusion: OntoPlan证明了本体论驱动的符号化场景表示和智能体框架在大规模机器人任务规划中的有效性，能够在保持高效token使用的同时实现高任务成功率，并能妥善处理模糊或不可行的指令。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based robot task planning is promising for open-ended instruction following, but degrades on long-horizon tasks in large environments. When spatial information is conveyed to the LLM through text, the model can fail to capture spatial context, and token cost grows with environment size. Generating action sequences directly with an LLM also makes it difficult to satisfy the current world state and action preconditions. We address this with an ontology-grounded scene representation that aligns objects, spaces, relations, and states in a shared symbolic vocabulary for spatial reasoning and task planning, and with OntoPlan, an agentic framework that interprets instructions, selectively retrieves task-relevant information, formalizes goals and constraints, and produces executable plans. Across 150 general tasks spanning five indoor environments and three scene scales, OntoPlan achieves 0.89 average task success, compared with 0.27 for the strongest baseline, while using 18.1k total tokens per task on average, about 5.6$\times$ fewer than the most efficient baseline. These advantages persist as scene scale increases, whereas prior methods degrade more sharply in success and remain far more costly in tokens. OntoPlan also responds appropriately to ambiguous or infeasible instructions by asking follow-up questions or reporting insufficient information rather than committing to invalid plans. Code available at https://github.com/namhyeongwoo/OntoPlan.

</details>


### [144] [Vector Map Quality Metrics for Contextual Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08043)
*Marie-Ngoïe Badibanga Kalenda,Philippe Bonnifait,Marie-Anne Mittet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GOSPAM的新指标，用于评估矢量地图质量并指导地图更新决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶的安全性需要持续地图维护和可靠的质量指标，以确定何时、何地以及何种条件下触发地图更新（如通过众包数据）。

Method: 提出GOSPAM指标，用于从位置误差、存在性和完整性三个维度测量地图偏差。通过对点要素和折线要素地图的详细仿真，分析其对常见地图退化（偏差、假阳性、假阴性、坐标误差）的敏感性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，GOSPAM能够有效捕捉各种地图偏差形式，提供统一且可解释的度量方法。

Conclusion: GOSPAM是自动驾驶应用中地图质量评估的有力候选指标。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in autonomous driving requires continuous map maintenance supported by reliable quality indicators. In this context, it is crucial to identify when and where map updates should be triggered, for instance through crowdsourced data, and under which conditions a new map compilation should be deployed. This paper focuses on effective metrics for assessing the quality of vector maps and guiding such decisions. We present a new metric called GOSPAM designed to measure map discrepancies in terms of location errors, existence, and completeness. Through detailed simulations on both point and polyline feature maps, we analyze its sensitivity to common map degradation such as bias, false positives, false negatives, and coordinate errors. The results demonstrate that GOSPAM offers a unified and interpretable measure that effectively captures various forms of map deviation, making it a strong candidate for map quality assessment in automotive applications.

</details>


### [145] [Mitigating Concept Drift in QoS Prediction for Teleoperation of Autonomous Vehicles Using Historic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08297)
*Xiyan Su,Jianning Gao,Mahmoud Ashri,Frank Diermeyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种面向远程操控的预测服务质量（pQoS）框架，旨在提高自动驾驶远程操控的鲁棒性。通过数据测量活动，预测了远程操控中两个关键网络KPI：上行数据速率和往返时延；引入了历史数据以缓解机器学习模型在未见数据上的性能退化；并提出了专门用于远程操控场景的临界场景检测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程操控作为自动驾驶的备选方案，其可靠性依赖于移动网络资源，而这些资源无法始终得到保证。为了提高远程操控的鲁棒性，引入了预测服务质量（pQoS）概念。

Method: 基于数据测量活动，提出预测框架用于预测上行数据速率和往返时延；引入方法将历史数据纳入预测流程以缓解概念漂移对机器学习模型性能的影响；提出临界场景检测指标以评估预测性能。

Result: 实现了对远程操控网络KPI的预测框架，并通过历史数据融合方法缓解了模型在未见数据上的性能下降。

Conclusion: 提出的临界场景检测指标为评估远程操控场景下的预测性能提供了专门的方法，有助于增强远程操控系统的可靠性和容错能力。

Abstract: Teleoperation serves as the fallback solution to autonomous driving but reliable functions of the teleoperation require a certain amount of mobile network resources, which cannot be guaranteed at all times. Therefore, predictive quality of service (pQoS) is introduced as a concept to increase the resilience of the teleoperation. In this paper, based on a data measurement campaign, we propose a prediction framework to prediction two important network KPIs of teleoperation: uplink data-rate and round-trip latency. Furthermore, we introduce a method to alleviate the performance degradation of machine-learning-based prediction models on previously unseen data due to concept drift by incorporating historic data into the prediction pipeline. Additionally, we introduce the metric of critical scenario detection to evaluate the prediction performance specifically for teleoperation.

</details>


### [146] [MIM-VLA: Learning Physical Interaction Representations from Gripper Motor Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08425)
*Jaeyoung Lee,Jiyeon Koo,Taehwa Kim,Yerin Cha,Andrew Jaeyong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MIM-VLA，一种基于电机反馈的VLA架构，通过128维交互令牌编码夹爪的电流、位置、速度和信号有效性，在SmolVLA夹爪动作路径上增加Motor Interaction Module（MIM）模块，利用预训练的接触和交互阶段标签进行条件控制，无需额外触觉阵列或力传感器即可提升物体交互阻力识别和易碎物体抓取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA策略主要从视觉观测和机器人状态推断抓取动作，但未显式表示接触后观察到的物理响应。为提升机器人对物体交互特性的理解能力和易碎物体的安全抓取，需要引入电机反馈来增强物理交互建模。

Method: MIM-VLA采用电机反馈架构，将夹爪的电流、位置、速度和信号有效性编码为128维交互令牌；Motor Interaction Module（MIM）仅包含电机部分，在人工标注的接触和交互阶段标签上预训练，仅条件化SmolVLA的夹爪动作路径，手臂动作和位置控制接口保持不变；交互令牌还支持MEM选择器VLM进行候选交互比较和证据条件选择。

Result: 在三个真实场景中，MIM-VLA在13个物体对测试中75.0%的试验正确选择了更高阻力的物体（基线SmolVLA为48.8%）；在区分真实与仿制品物体、安全抓取易碎物体等任务上均表现出优于基线的性能。

Conclusion: MIM-VLA仅需利用夹爪已有的电机反馈信息，无需额外的触觉阵列、力矩传感器、校准力估计或直接电流控制，即可有效提升机器人对物体交互特性和易碎物体的抓取能力，为实际部署提供了一种轻量且实用的方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies infer grasp actions primarily from visual observations and robot state, but do not explicitly represent the physical response observed after contact. We present MIM-VLA, a motor-feedback-based architecture that encodes recent gripper current, position, velocity, and signal validity as a 128-dimensional interaction token. A motor-only Motor Interaction Module (MIM) is pretrained with human-reviewed contact and interaction-phase labels and then conditions only the gripper-action pathway of SmolVLA; arm actions and the position-control interface remain unchanged. The same token supports the MEM selector VLM that compares candidate interactions and produces evidence-conditioned selections and explanations. We evaluate MIM-VLA in three real-world settings: comparing the interaction resistance of visually different objects, disambiguating visually similar real and replica objects through active probing, and gently grasping fragile objects, including held-out instances. Across 13 object pairs, MIM-VLA selects the higher-resistance object in 75.0% of trials, compared with 48.8% for the SmolVLA baseline. For the evaluated tasks, the approach uses motor feedback already available from the gripper and does not require an additional tactile array, force-torque sensor, calibrated force estimate, or direct current control.

</details>


### [147] [RIWANav: Recursive World-Action Models with Self-Improvement for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08640)
*Jing Xie,Shouwei Ruan,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Haitao Yang,Songchang Jin,Dianxi Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RIWANAV是一个用于长期城市导航的框架，通过策略与世界模型的递归自我改进循环，在无需物理试错的情况下实现持续性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期城市导航任务中，顺序局部决策误差会随时间累积。模仿学习难以从失败中学习，而物理试错强化学习成本高昂。现有世界模型在策略演化后可靠性下降，缺乏有效的协同优化机制。

Method: RIWANAV采用递归自我改进（RSI）框架，每个循环包含两个更新步骤：1) 世界模型通过想象结果评估策略动作，提供比较反馈用于组相对策略优化（GRPO）；2) 改进后的策略构建 grounded 自主课程，通过行为新颖性和预测误差选择专家一致的动作-视频对；精炼的世界模型为下一次策略更新提供反馈，形成闭环。

Result: 实验显示RIWANAV优于训练基线和先前方法，验证了策略与世界模型间的递归自我改进循环。实地试验进一步证实其实际应用价值。

Conclusion: RIWANAV成功证明了策略与世界模型协同递归自我改进的可行性，为长期城市导航提供了高效、低成本的学习解决方案，具有广泛的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Long-horizon urban navigation requires sequential local decisions whose errors can compound over time. Imitation learning (IL) rarely learns from failures, while physical trial-and-error reinforcement learning (RL) is costly. Action-conditioned world models can provide imagined feedback by predicting visual consequences for candidate actions. However, a frozen world model may become less reliable as the policy evolves. In this paper, we introduce RIWANAV, a post-training framework that casts the coupled adaptation of a world model and an action model (policy) as task-specific recursive self-improvement (RSI). Each cycle alternates two updates. The world model evaluates policy actions through imagined outcomes, providing comparative feedback for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO). The improved policy then constructs a grounded self-curriculum, selecting expert-consistent action-video pairs by behavioral novelty and prediction error. The refined world model supplies feedback for the next policy update, closing the recursive self-improvement loop. Experiments show that RIWANAV outperforms training baselines and prior methods, validating the proposed recursive self-improvement loop between the policy and world model. Real-world trials further demonstrate its practical applicability.

</details>


### [148] [HygieneRoboBench: Benchmarking Hygiene-Aware Planning for Household Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08642)
*Yurun Chen,Josh Qixuan Sun,Jason Qin,Chengtai Li,Tianyi Wang,Mark Crowley,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HygieneRoboBench基准测试及Hygiene-NSP规划器，用于评估家庭机器人处理接触污染和卫生风险的能力，在用户优先级约束下实现安全任务完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法联合评估规划器如何从接触历史识别卫生风险并在新接触事件后规划安全延续，且规划器需在时间、资源限制和用户优先级约束下完成任务。

Method: HygieneRoboBench包含624个实例的134个任务家族，通过双夹爪和共享对象模拟污染、治疗成本和用户优先级。评估采用控制历史、配置和事件比较与独立规划评估。Hygiene-NSP结合LLM基础、接触历史重构和CP-SAT，联合规划卫生处理与任务执行。

Result: Hygiene-NSP在安全解决率和最优安全解决率上分别达到94.4%和90.4%，均高于评估的基线规划器。安全完成任务并不保证在用户优先级下执行成本最低。

Conclusion: 安全解决任务不等于最优成本，Hygiene-NSP通过LLM基础、历史重构和CP-SAT有效解决该问题，在基准测试中取得最优性能。

Abstract: Contact with contaminated objects can spread hazards through a household robot's grippers, tools, and shared surfaces, while new contacts can make an existing plan unsafe. Existing benchmarks do not jointly assess how planners identify hygiene risks from contact history and plan safe continuations after new contact events. Planners must do so within time and resource limits while respecting user priorities. We introduce HygieneRoboBench, with 624 instances across 134 task families, to evaluate safe resolution of household tasks from a given execution history. Tasks capture contamination through two grippers and shared objects, treatment costs, and user priorities. We combine controlled history, profile, and event comparisons with independent plan evaluation. These assess safe resolution, cost efficiency under user priorities, and responses to contact events. Evaluation of LLM-based and symbolic planners shows that safely completing a task does not guarantee the lowest execution costs under the user's priorities. To address this problem, we introduce Hygiene-NSP. It combines LLM-based grounding, contact-history reconstruction, and CP-SAT to jointly plan hygiene treatment and task execution under user priorities. Hygiene-NSP achieves safe resolution and optimal safe resolution rates of 94.4% and 90.4%, respectively. Both rates are higher than those of the evaluated baseline planners on the full dataset. Project page: https://euron-zc.github.io/HygieneRoboBench/.

</details>


### [149] [PEARS: Physical-Prior-Guided Efficient Adaptation via Failure Reasoning and Diffusion Steering for Tactile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08784)
*Kun Song,Yiming Wang,Yilin Chen,Tianyi Ding,Jiaxin Tian,Tianqi Gong,Daolin Ma,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Pretrained robotic policies can suffer substantial performance degradation under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions encountered during deployment, motivating post-training through real-world interaction. However, reinforcement-learning (RL)-based post-training typically requires substantial environment interactions, a burden that is especially significant in manipulation, where each trial can be slow, costly, or destructive. Therefore, we present PEARS, a physics-prior-guided hybrid RL framework for sample-efficient online adaptation of pretrained policies with tactile feedback. After each episode, its physics-guided force reasoning (PFR) module uses physical priors encoded in a vision-language model (VLM) to diagnose failures from the visual outcome and tactile interaction history and update task-appropriate contact-force bounds. A high-frequency hybrid force-position controller then enforces these bounds during contact. Complementarily, tactile-conditioned diffusion steering reinforcement learning adjusts the latent noise of the frozen flow-matching policy to correct errors in free-space motion and contact timing without updating the base model. In simulation, PEARS improves success rates by 12.4-37.4 percentage points over the strongest per-task baselines. PEARS also reduces the number of interaction episodes required for a certain success threshold by up to 53.2% relative to the fastest baseline. In real-world experiments, PEARS achieves success rates of 95% on Whiteboard Erasing and 90% on Pipette Liquid Aspiration. These results show that combining the PFR module with policy steering can accelerate adaptation while reducing costly interactions. The project website is available at https://song-kun.github.io/pears.

</details>
